**Verdict, both reviewers in.** [r523](../reviews/REVIEW_r523.md) and [r524](../reviews/REVIEW_r524.md), identical briefs, **both CONFIRMED**, each rebuilding the collapse from the definitions. **The headline force holds**: the complete input list, $\lVert I\rVert^2=\binom n2$, $I\#I=(n-2)I$, $W\#I=-W$, $\mathrm{ric}(Q(W))=0$, and the cone coefficients, contains **no sphere-product computation and nothing downstream of $\Theta_n$**, so $\theta_0(n)$ is forced from outside the family it describes. Unlike [L2](problem.md), this route has no sphere product in it at all.

**And the collapse is stronger than the block claimed.** The cancellation of $\lVert\mathrm{ric}(I)\rVert^2$ and of the $c^3(n-1)\lVert I\rVert^2$ term never uses the boundary equation, so **it is an identity on the whole slice**, with $\partial\Omega_0$ entering only to fix $c$.

**The reviewers divided once, and the record settles it against the one that looked.** [r523](../reviews/REVIEW_r523.md) reports the record keeping Einstein and Ricci-flat apart correctly everywhere it checked; [r524](../reviews/REVIEW_r524.md) found [results.md](../results.md) naming the Ricci-flat slice. **The line exists**, and the defect is sharper than a mislabel: Ricci-flatness is $c=0$, where the boundary forces $\lVert W\rVert=0$, so that copy stated as its hypothesis the one value $c$ cannot take.

**The printed cone was missing a clause that makes it false.** $\Omega_a$ carries $\operatorname{scal}(R)>0$ in the source's own definition, and $F$ is even in $R$, so without it the sublevel set is a double cone whose $c<0$ nappe reads $\langle W,Q(W)\rangle\le-\theta_0(n)\lVert W\rVert^3$, **violated by every sphere product**. Restored in both copies, which also licenses the positive root of $c$ that the derivation had been taking silently.

**Two citation repairs.** A degree error, $\lVert W\rVert^2$ where the cubic needs $\lVert W\rVert^3$; and an over-reading crediting L43's bridge for "$P$ and Conjecture A are the same statement", which [problem.md](problem.md) denies in terms.

### L27, independent validation of r530, while r529 runs

**Two checks the block never makes, both run here and both closing.** The ten dimensions sum to $\dim\mathcal W_n$ exactly at every split tested, $(3,3)$ through $(5,6)$; and **$\operatorname{tr}L_e=0$ identically in $p,q$**, which is forced because $\mathcal W_n$ is $O(n)$-irreducible and non-trivial. No arithmetic slip in a ten-term list produces a vanishing trace by accident.

**And the trace discriminates the one assignment the record disagrees with itself about.** With $+\mu$ on $\Lambda^2\otimes\Lambda^2$ and $-\mu$ on $S^2_0\otimes S^2_0$ (the block's reading), the trace vanishes. **Reversed, it is $30$ at $(3,5)$ and $(4,4)$, $48$ at $(5,5)$, $70$ at $(5,7)$: never zero.** So [nondegeneracy.md](nondegeneracy.md) line 686, which pairs $+\mu$ with the symmetric summand, is **wrong**, and the block is right, as [r527](../reviews/REVIEW_r527.md) also found from the tables. Nothing cites line 686.

**A gap this ledger recorded an hour ago is closed by the same identity.** [r528](../reviews/REVIEW_r528.md) noted that the $-\mu$ on $S^2_0\otimes S^2_0$ had no source, [L26](nondegeneracy.md) supplying only the $\Lambda^2\otimes\Lambda^2$ eigenvector. **$\operatorname{tr}L_e=0$ forces it uniquely**, and forces $\lambda_m=\frac{m-2}{2(m-1)}$ with it.

**The brief's own worry was unfounded, and it is worth recording as such.** Multiplicity-freeness holds at $p=q$; the summands that looked like collision candidates are distinguished as $O(p)\times O(q)$-representations rather than as $O(m)$-representations.

**One wording defect stands.** *"Since $L_e$ is symmetric the $\mathbb C$ and $\mathbb H$ Schur cases cannot occur"* is a non sequitur, symmetry of an operator cannot change a representation's type. The right statement uses symmetry differently: a non-real element of $\mathbb C$ or $\mathbb H$ has no real eigenvalue, so a symmetric element of the commutant is real whichever case holds. **The conclusion is untouched, and this file already carries the sharper phrasing thirty lines below.**

**Verdict, both reviewers in: CONFIRMED, and the record's most-cited result stands.** [r529](../reviews/REVIEW_r529.md) and [r530](../reviews/REVIEW_r530.md), identical briefs, agreeing on all six attack lines. **Both derived $\operatorname{tr}_{\mathcal W_n}L_e=0$ independently and concurrently**, neither having read the other, and it is now in the block: the ten values satisfy it identically in $p,q$, it forces the one value the block had sourced wrongly, and it discriminates the assignment the file was unsure of.

**[Lemma L3](second-order.md)'s promotion and [Lemma L69](graded-half.md)'s nondegeneracy half both sit strictly inside L27's scope**, so the two results closed on it today keep their support.

**The brief's own worry was unfounded and the reviewers said why.** Multiplicity-freeness holds at $p=q$: the collision candidates are separated by the *external* tensor structure, $([2],[\,])$ against $([\,],[2])$, and the $m=3$ dimension ties by the $\det$-twist.

**One reported defect could not be located and is not repaired.** Three reviewers across two results describe a passage in this file pairing $+\mu$ with the symmetric summand $S^2_0\otimes S^2_0$. **A sweep of every findings file finds no such line**, the only occurrence of that pairing is this ledger's own description of it, and [nondegeneracy.md](nondegeneracy.md) line 716 has the assignment right. *Either it was repaired before this audit reached it or the line numbers the reviewers quoted had drifted; a repair is not invented for a defect that cannot be found.*

**Two repairs applied.** The Schur step's stated reason was a non sequitur (symmetry cannot change a representation's type) and is replaced by the argument that does the work: a symmetric element of a real division algebra of operators has a real eigenvalue and is therefore a real scalar, covering all three cases at once. And the $-\mu$ on $S^2_0\otimes S^2_0$, which the block credited to [L26](nondegeneracy.md) and L26 does not supply, is now credited to the trace identity that forces it.

Eighth result this session whose own file corrects it below the block**, and here the correction is 47 lines down |
| a miscitation in the plan | [plan.md](../plan.md) credits L121 for "$\mathcal D_n$ being a linear subspace"; that is a definition, not this lemma | **holds** |

**The cross-check with [L26](nondegeneracy.md) is exact on three counts**, and it is the strongest evidence either result has. Only $(2,2)$-splits are nonzero, since $(4,0)$ and $(3,1)$ force $A=B=C$; the value is $\frac{n-2}{2(p-1)(q-1)}$; and the $+\rho$ eigenvector $(2,-1,-1)$ in signed coordinates is $(2,1,-1)$ raw, which is L26's $\tfrac23,\tfrac13,-\tfrac13$ with its $\lVert X\rVert^2=\tfrac43$. **Two closed forms derived by unrelated routes meeting on the eigenvector as well as the eigenvalue.** L26's $(3,4)$ exception concerns which eigenvalue is second-largest and does not touch this.

**Verdict, both reviewers in: CONFIRMED.** [r531](../reviews/REVIEW_r531.md) and [r532](../reviews/REVIEW_r532.md), identical briefs, agreeing on every point, each deriving $G$ from the structure constants and each independently correcting the brief's false premise about its symmetry.

**The agreement with [Lemma L26](nondegeneracy.md) is a theorem, and that is the audit's strongest single result.** It reduces to $\theta=(n-1)\lvert\gamma\rvert$, which in $s=p+q$ and $m=pq$ is $m(s^3-4s^2+5s-2)=m(s-1)^2(s-2)$, **an identity, verified here**. So two closed forms derived by wholly unrelated routes agree at every $(p,q)$, on the eigenvector as well as the eigenvalue: $(2,-1,-1)$ in signed coordinates is L26's $\tfrac23,\tfrac13,-\tfrac13$ raw, with its $\lVert X\rVert^2=\tfrac43$. **Each is now strong evidence for the other.**

**Three presentational repairs.** The hypothesis was over-specified, the block and spectrum need diagonality alone, which this file already said 47 lines below, and only the necessary condition needs a maximiser. The Bianchi implication ran backwards: $\mathbf1^\perp$ **is** the Bianchi subspace because the signed coordinates sum to zero, and $G\mathbf1=(A+B+C)\mathbf1$ buys its **invariance**, which is a second joint. And "the block is traceless" holds of the restriction to $\mathbf1^\perp$, not of $\tfrac12G$, whose trace is $\tfrac12(A+B+C)$, $-0.2108$ at $S^3\times S^3$.

**The constant is right and sharp.** $L_M=\tfrac12G$ against [L24](second-order.md)'s $\lambda_{\max}(L_M\vert_{W^\perp})\le\theta/2$ cancels to $\Sigma\le\theta^2$, with **equality at $S^3\times S^3$**, so a lost factor of two would have been visible rather than hidden. No citation is rescaled, and [plan.md](../plan.md)'s credit to L121 for $\mathcal D_n$ being a linear subspace is a definition's work, now attributed there.

### L126, verdict, both reviewers: CONFIRMED

[r533](../reviews/REVIEW_r533.md) and [r534](../reviews/REVIEW_r534.md), identical briefs, agreeing, each deriving $\mu_{p,q}$ and $DF(M)$ from the definitions after calibrating against the record's anchors.

**The brief's lead suspicion was wrong for the third time today, and both reviewers gave the same reason.** The cut of $\langle M\rangle$ is not owed here: $L_e$ is self-adjoint with $L_eM=M$, so the $\tfrac12$-eigenspace lies in $M^\perp$ **automatically**, and the kernel claim holds on the ambient space as well as the tangent one, stronger than the block states. **[Lemma L120](second-order.md) needs its cut because it bounds $\lambda_{\max}$ over a block containing $M$; L126 names a single eigenvalue $\tfrac12\ne1$.** *Three of this session's briefs have now planted a suspicion the reviewers had to spend attention refuting; each was generated from reading a statement rather than from deriving it.*

**The scope turns out to be the exact complement of [L27](nondegeneracy.md)'s.** That lemma is stated for $p,q\ge3$ with $(p,q)\ne(3,3)$; L126's content is $p=2$ and $(3,3)$. **Between them the two-factor family is covered with no overlap and no gap**, a division of labour the record had left implicit, now stated.

**The repair that matters is a claim that lost its qualifications in transit.** L126 read "**At $S^3\times S^3$ no branch emanates**"; its source records the constancy of $\Psi_1$ **across 300 samples** and closes by leaving open the Puiseux arcs of leading exponent $\ge2$, which contribute order-three cross terms it does not exclude. Both qualifications are restored. **The logical direction was sound; what travelled was the conclusion without its conditions.**

**Two smaller repairs and one gain.** Unit norm is now stated, which $P(M)=\theta$ requires; the Diophantine step gains its missing line, $(u-1)(v-1)=1$; and [r533](../reviews/REVIEW_r533.md) contributes the padded four-set value $\frac1{2(p-1)}$, exactly $\tfrac12$ at $p=2$ for every $q$, **so padding moves $S^2\times S^q$ from above the threshold onto it**, which is why the block's closing caveat must stay.

**One item is left unverified under the analysis-only rule**, and both reviewers named it rather than running it: the no-branch claim rests on a numerical constant, and nothing else in L126 moves on it.

### L127, independent validation of r535, while r536 runs

**The cross-check the brief asked for closes exactly, and it is the strongest thing in the review.** [L27](nondegeneracy.md) decomposes $L_e$ by $O(p)\times O(q)$ representation and L127 by sign-character block family (two unrelated decompositions of one operator) and they agree on the spectrum **with multiplicity, identically in $p,q$**. Both identities verified here symbolically:

$$(p-1)+\binom p2=\dim S^2_0(\mathbb R^p),\qquad \binom p2\binom q2+(p-1)(q-1)+\binom p2(q-1)+\binom q2(p-1)=\dim\bigl(S^2_0\otimes S^2_0\bigr).$$

**Six defects, of which two were found by earlier reviewers and never folded in.** [r375](../reviews/REVIEW_r375.md) found the within-factor top unattained at $p=2$, where the block is $(-\mu)^{q-1}$ entire; [r392](../reviews/REVIEW_r392.md) recommended the very repair the $\mathcal D_n$ row still needs. *A finding filed and not applied is a finding the record does not have.*

**L127 inherits [L120](second-order.md)'s cut and does not handle it.** Its $\mathcal D_n$ row prints top $=1$ under a column headed "top $L_e$-eigenvalue" and then answers "no" to "reaches $\tfrac12$?", consistent only after $\langle e\rangle$ is removed, which the block never says. **Two consumers already read that row two different ways**, and a third site silently writes $\mathcal D_n\cap(W^*)^\perp$, applying the repair without stating it.

**The column asks $=\tfrac12$, and every row is consistent under that reading**, but [L24](second-order.md) needs $\ge\tfrac12$, and under *that* reading the four-set row's "only at $(3,3)$" is false at $\min(p,q)=2$, where $\mu_{2,q}>\tfrac12$. [L126](second-order.md)'s own box states this in bold, so the two blocks disagree unless the column's sense is written down.

**And one of the brief's questions rested on a false premise, which the reviewer said plainly.** The pair row states a fact about the block rather than a bound, using [L122](second-order.md)'s Kirchhoff rewriting (diagonality and Ricci-flatness, both present) and never touching the inequality that needs a maximiser. *Nothing owed.* **That is now four briefs in a row carrying an error of mine**, though this one also carried the question whose answer is the review's best result.

**Verdict, both reviewers in.** [r535](../reviews/REVIEW_r535.md) **PLAUSIBLE** and [r536](../reviews/REVIEW_r536.md) **CONFIRMED** at $p,q\ge3$, agreeing on the mathematics and on every defect. **Both closed the cross-check independently**, and [r536](../reviews/REVIEW_r536.md) sharpens what it proves: the $\mathcal D_n$ row is **forced** by [L27](nondegeneracy.md) together with the other three rows, not merely compatible with them. Closing it required the within-factor block's whole spectrum $\{\lambda_p,(-\mu)^{q-1},0^{p-3}\}$, which the table does not print.

**The reviewers divided on whether the cut propagates, and the record settles it for [r536](../reviews/REVIEW_r536.md).** [r535](../reviews/REVIEW_r535.md) read two consumers as taking the $\mathcal D_n$ row two different ways; checking the disputed one, [ATTEMPTS.md](../ATTEMPTS.md) line 3978 says the bound "comes from the granted diagonal case rather than from a computed spectrum", **it supplies the cut itself and knows the row's limits.** So L127 sidesteps [L120](second-order.md)'s omitted clause by asking $=\tfrac12$, and nothing propagates.

**The live defect is the within-factor row at $p=2$**, where the $\lambda_p$-eigenvector needs $F_1\setminus\{j,k\}$ nonempty: the block is entirely $-\mu$, so the printed top is not in the spectrum at all, $(-\tfrac23)^3$ at $S^2\times S^4$ against a stated $0$. **[r375](../reviews/REVIEW_r375.md) found this and it was never folded in**, as [r392](../reviews/REVIEW_r392.md)'s repair for the $\mathcal D_n$ row's source was not. *Two findings that existed in the record and did not reach the block they were about.*

**Four repairs applied**: the range on the within-factor row, the column's sense written down, a forward pointer to [Lemma L128](second-order.md), which reinstates the branching clause L127 withdraws, and a broken post link. **And the cross-check is now in the block**, where it makes the table self-verifying rather than asserted.

**Verdict, both reviewers in: PLAUSIBLE.** [r537](../reviews/REVIEW_r537.md) and [r538](../reviews/REVIEW_r538.md), identical briefs, agreeing on every finding. Parts (i)–(iii) confirmed by cold derivation; part (iv)'s statement is true and one step of its proof is not.

**The circular step, and it is not needed.** *"$A$ is closed in the compact $S$, hence compact"*, but $A$ is cut by the **open** complement of $B$, so nothing elementary closes it, and "$A$ is closed" is equivalent to the conclusion $\overline A\cap B=\varnothing$ being proved. **Taking the closure repairs it in one clause**: $\overline A$ is compact for free and disjoint compacts give $d(A,B)\ge d(\overline A,B)>0$.

**Three things are stronger than the block states.** Part (i) holds at **any** frame-diagonal $W$, criticality being unused. **No part needs maximality**, the reverse of this brief's worry. And the isolation genuinely *follows from* the nondegeneracy through an ambient implicit-function argument, with no slice, rather than being asserted alongside it.

**Both reviewers reached the [L11](nondegeneracy.md) refutation independently**, [r537](../reviews/REVIEW_r537.md) by deriving the whole $S^2\times S^4$ spectrum by hand, which also **localises the record's known $86$-against-$84$ over-count to $S^2_0(\mathbb R^2)$**, a figure the record had carried without a reason until [r534](../reviews/REVIEW_r534.md) explained it this afternoon and [r537](../reviews/REVIEW_r537.md) placed it.

**Four bookkeeping repairs.** [plan.md](../plan.md) cited L128(ii) for a Łojasiewicz exponent it does not contain, the lemma supplies that result's *hypothesis*; four anchors under two stale titles are repointed; and [Corollary L128.1](second-order.md)'s headline said "every two-block sphere product" where its body assumes $p,q\ge3$, a scope [r375](../reviews/REVIEW_r375.md)'s repair reached the body of and not the headline.

**Two of this brief's questions rested on false premises**, and both reviewers said so: the block has **four** parts rather than three, and it has no use for [L37](target.md), since it hypothesises a frame rather than deciding one exists. *Five briefs running.*

### L9, independent validation of r539, while r540 runs

**The refutation holds on the record's own table.** [target.md](target.md) line 560 gives $\mathbb{CP}^3$ at $n=6$: $\theta=1.054093$, $\lambda_{\max}(L_e\vert_{e^\perp})=0.62500000$, an **8**-dimensional violating subspace, and the verdict "**violates**". Line 579 gives its excess as **$0$**, $\dim E(\tfrac12)=6=$ the orbit.

**So L9's second paragraph is false on both of its counts.** It calls that point a "*third-order* saddle, second variation negative semidefinite, but the value rises cubically along the extra flat directions". The second variation is **not** semidefinite, $\lambda_{\max}=0.625>\tfrac12$ putting an $8$-dimensional strictly positive subspace in $6\theta(L_e-\tfrac12\mathrm{Id})$; and there are **no extra flat directions** for a cubic to rise along, the excess being zero.

**The description is verbatim the record's profile of $S^3\times S^3$ at $n=6$** (excess $9$, $P$ rising at cubic order) which this audit re-established this afternoon when [L3](second-order.md) was refuted at that very point. **And the two share $\theta$ exactly**: $\theta_0(6)=\sqrt{10}/3=1.054093$, which is $\mathbb{CP}^3$'s recorded value to the digit. *Verified here.* **The file already warns that $\theta$ does not predict the verdict and that these two land on opposite sides; L9 is on the wrong side of its own file's warning.**

**And "$n=12$" stands on neither reading.** As $\mathbb{HP}^3$ it heads the same file's list of points that do not saturate, so it is not a local maximum at all. As padded $\mathbb{HP}^2$ the block omits the word "padded", drops a row of the table it draws on, and rests on $\lambda_{\max}\not>\tfrac12$, **the necessary condition read as the sufficient one, which is exactly [L3](second-order.md)'s refuted move**, made twice in one file on one afternoon.

**What survives is the headline and a promotion.** "More than one peak" is confirmed. And $\mathbb{HP}^2$ at $n=8$ is **provable**: it is $\tau(S^3\times S^5)$ for $\tau$ orthogonal, so conjugation preserves $Q$, fixes $\mathcal W_8$ and carries orbits to orbits; [L27](nondegeneracy.md) at $(3,5)$ gives $E(\tfrac12)=$ the orbit tangent exactly, and transporting it with [L24](second-order.md) gives strict local maximality transverse to the orbit, **the same shape of argument that promoted [L3](second-order.md) today**.

**Blast radius nil.** All five live citations take the $n=8$ claim or an independent value argument; the false paragraph was never copied out of this file. Held for [r540](../reviews/REVIEW_r540.md) before applying, since a promotion on a supplied proof is where a second reader matters most.

**Verdict, both reviewers in.** [r539](../reviews/REVIEW_r539.md) **REFUTED** and [r540](../reviews/REVIEW_r540.md) **PLAUSIBLE and under-claimed**, agreeing on every point of content and differing only on where to set the label. **Both reached the same triality proof independently.**

**The governing fact, which [r540](../reviews/REVIEW_r540.md) states and which reaches past this result.** By [Lemma L24](second-order.md) the orbit directions are exact $\tfrac12$-eigenvectors at **every** critical point with $\theta\ne0$, so $\lambda_{\max}(L_e\vert_{W^\perp})\ge\tfrac12$ always and **a negative semidefinite Hessian is automatic**. The whole content of a local-maximality claim is therefore the multiplicity, $\dim E(\tfrac12)\cap W^\perp=\dim T_W\mathcal O$, **and L9 stated none, at any of its three points, at any dimension.** *That is the failure that refuted [Observation L3](second-order.md) earlier the same day, and it is now written into this block rather than rediscovered a third time.*

**What is promoted.** $\mathbb{HP}^2$ at $n=8$ is $\tau(S^3\times S^5)$ for triality $\tau$, an orthogonal automorphism preserving bracket and inner product hence $Q$, and fixing $\mathcal W_8$ since the complement's three $35$s are permuted. [L27](nondegeneracy.md) at $(3,5)$ gives $\dim E(\tfrac12)=15=\dim\mathcal O$; $\tau$ normalises the inner automorphisms, so the identity transfers. **Strict local maximality, proved**, the third promotion of the day on the L24-plus-L27 pattern.

**What is refuted.** "$n=12$" stands on neither reading. And the $\mathbb{CP}^3$ paragraph described a different point: it is an ordinary saddle with an $8$-dimensional violating subspace and excess $0$, while the description (semidefinite, rising cubically along flat directions), is the record's profile of $S^3\times S^3$. **The two share $\theta=\sqrt{10}/3=1.054093$ exactly**, and the same file warns that $\theta$ does not predict the verdict.

**Three citation repairs.** The $\mathbb{CP}^m$ closed form was credited to [L8](graded-half.md), downgraded today and whose refutation names $\mathbb{CP}^m$ at $m\ge4$ among its failures; [L10](graded-half.md) carries it with Gate 2 passed. **L8's own heading still read `Lemma` in two files while the index read `Conjecture`**, a gap left by this audit's own downgrade this morning, now closed. And the results.md copy reproduced the false paragraph verbatim, $n=12$ and all.

### L72, verdict, both reviewers

[r541](../reviews/REVIEW_r541.md) **CONFIRMED** and [r542](../reviews/REVIEW_r542.md) **REFUTED on the closure clause**, agreeing on both halves. **The forward implication is exact**, spectrum $(2,-1,-1)/\sqrt6$, $\theta=\sqrt{3/2}$, $T=2$ at every critical point of the $(1,2)$ stratum, every $n\ge4$, and each reviewer recovered $\operatorname{tr}W^\#=-1$ at the conclusion, so the point is genuinely critical rather than formally so.

**The block's route is stronger than the parent's.** This session's brief derived $a=b$ by subtracting two rows and using $T\ge0$, $\lambda_1>0$ and $\theta>0$; the block instead feeds the second branch into row (ii) and gets $T=-ab/\lambda_1^2<0$ against $T=c^2$. **That needs no sign hypothesis on $\theta$ at all**, and $\theta=3a>0$ is a *conclusion*, so no $\theta<0$ critical point exists in this stratum and [L29](stratum-two-negative.md)'s neighbouring sign trap does not reach here.

**The closure claim inverts a containment, and this audit propagated it.** L72 quantifies over **critical** points; the clause it claims to close quantifies over **equality** points; and by [L29](stratum-two-negative.md) together with L72 the critical points are a **subset** of the equality points. **So L72 is the weaker statement and closes nothing.** What the numerics at $r=6,8,10$ hedged was the realisability of a full-support rank-$3$ element, which the criticality rows do not touch.

**The clause is closed, in three lines, by machinery a hundred lines above it**: equality forces $T_{1,N-1,N}=2$, hence $\lVert[v_{N-1},v_N]\rVert^2=2$, hence $\operatorname{im}v_2=\operatorname{im}v_3$ of dimension $4$ by [Lemma L31](stratum-two-negative.md), hence $r=4$ through Ricci-flatness. [Lemma L33](stratum-two-negative.md) closes it by a second route.

**Five sites carried the misattribution, and two of them this audit wrote today**, the [L29](stratum-two-negative.md) repair and this ledger's own record of it, both made two hours ago on the block's word. *The same file was carrying three different statuses for one clause: "reduced but not closed", "reduced but open", and "closed by Lemma L72".* Corrected to [L31](stratum-two-negative.md) at three of the five, **and this sentence's claim to have corrected all of them was false for four months of rounds**: [results.md](../results.md):441 and :784 still carried the withdrawn credit until [ROUND_L29](../verification/ROUND_L29.md) reached them, and those two are the sites a consumer reads. *The repair is also not a name swap*: the weak converse (equality forces the spectrum), follows in two lines from L29's own equality case and needs neither lemma; L31 supplies the strong one, that equality forces support $r=4$.

**This is the third correction today to a repair this audit itself made**, after [L11](nondegeneracy.md)'s exceptions and the [L26](nondegeneracy.md) near-miss. Each time the failure was the same: **a claim was checked for internal soundness and not against what it quantifies over.**

### L31, verdict, both reviewers: CONFIRMED

[r543](../reviews/REVIEW_r543.md) and [r544](../reviews/REVIEW_r544.md), identical briefs, agreeing on the mathematics and on every defect. **L31 is true at every $n$, in both directions, and the direction its new consumers need is the sound one**, which matters because it acquired [L29](stratum-two-negative.md)'s converse an hour ago, when [L72](stratum-two-negative.md) was found to quantify over the smaller set.

**The cap and its equality case come from one computation**, so no consumer inherits a hypothesis: $\max_{\lVert Y\rVert=1}\lVert[X,Y]\rVert^2=(s_1+s_2)^2$ and $(s_1+s_2)^2\le2\sum s_i^2=2$, with equality forcing exactly two equal singular values. **The three conditions are independent**, the sharpest witness being a self-dual and an anti-self-dual structure on $\mathbb R^4$: orthonormal, both rank $4$ with singular values $1/\sqrt2$, equal images, **and commuting**, bracket zero. So anticommuting cannot be deduced from the rest, and nothing is missing.

**A withdrawn number was standing ten lines above its own refutation**, because the sentence retiring it was deleted in a refactor. The passage measures an *ascent towards* the cap and reports ranks $4$ and $6$ at $n=8$, which the cap forbids, both matrices being rank $4$ there with equal images, so the common kernel would be $4$-dimensional against the $2$ measured. **The figures are a rank-tolerance artefact of the ascent**, and the block now says so. *A correction that existed and was removed is a new failure mode for this audit's list.*

**Two dead anchors sat on exactly the two sentences the closure is reached through**, both pointing at an old title, "and what saturates it", which belongs to a different lemma. **One of them this session wrote an hour ago.** So did the index slip at the same site, which mixed $T_{1,N-1,N}$ with $\lVert[v_2,v_3]\rVert^2$; it reads $\lVert[v_{N-1},v_N]\rVert^2$ now, here and in this ledger.

**Three smaller repairs.** The provenance line was false again, this file having carried the whole argument before the move; rank $4$ was derived for $X$ with the "symmetric in $X$ and $Y$, so both" clause dropped from the canonical block; and a downstream site used "rank $\le8$" where L31 gives rank exactly $4$. **[conventions.md](conventions.md) filed the pair cap as a standing fact with no pointer to the lemma proving it**, which is now supplied.

### L58, independent validation of r546, while r545 runs

**The statement is true and the mechanism is stronger than the block uses.** $W_{\mathfrak h}=c(\Pi_{\mathfrak h}-\tfrac rN\mathrm{Id})$ is affine in a projector, so its spectrum is the affine image of $\{1,0\}$, exactly two eigenvalues at $0<r<N$, with $\mathfrak h$ recoverable as an eigenspace. **Three distinct eigenvalues therefore excludes every $\mathfrak h$ at once**, which is more than the block's third row asks for.

| the Jensen witness fails | its only gradedness evidence is $S=0$, and [graded-half.md](graded-half.md) line 366 files that screen as sign-graded and "**Vacuous wherever $\ker W\ne0$**", the point has a $7$-dimensional Weyl kernel | **holds** |
| the $S^5\times S^7$ row evaluates the family formula at $r=31$ | above the cap $r\le30$ that [L23](ungraded-slack.md) proves at $n=12$, and which that lemma states of this very subalgebra | **holds as an infelicity**, though the row's conclusion is right for a better reason, the eigenvalue count alone settles it |
| the pointer is truncation, not absence | the heading ends "at every threshold" and the section holds the argument | **holds. Fourth pointer flagged today, second that merely lost its tail** |

**Two witnesses carry all three legs and either alone proves the lemma**, $S^5\times S^7$, a pair-graded symmetric pair, critical, spectrum $(42,20,-24)$ with $\theta^2=264/175$; and $\mathbb{CP}^3$, graded in all three of the record's senses, critical, three eigenvalues. **So the result stands and the block needs a column back**, not an argument.

**Verdict, both reviewers in.** [r545](../reviews/REVIEW_r545.md) **CONFIRMED** on two of three witnesses and [r546](../reviews/REVIEW_r546.md) **PLAUSIBLE**, agreeing on the mathematics, on the failing witness and on every defect. **The lemma is true and the block did not establish it.**

**Two things had to be put back before the block said what its heading claims.** The **gradedness column** was dropped in the paste from the report, so the table gave three points outside the family and asserted gradedness of nothing; and **the table was raw LaTeX inside a markdown file, so none of it rendered.** The evidence for the headline was simultaneously absent and invisible. **The fourth witness, $W_{\mathbb{CP}^2}$ (the point that anchors the threshold sentence the block kept), was dropped in the same move**, and is restored.

**One witness is withdrawn.** The $(\mathfrak{su}(3),\mathfrak{so}(3))$ point's only evidence of gradedness is $S=0$, and [trap W4](INDEX-traps.md) marks that screen vacuous wherever $\ker W\ne0$, this point's Weyl kernel is $7$-dimensional. **The source section said exactly that**, that calling it graded outright overstates what was measured, and the caveat was stripped in transit; this file then asserted "$S=0$, **graded**" thirty lines below its own retraction, both now corrected. **Either surviving witness proves the lemma alone.**

**The mechanism is stronger than the block used it.** $W_{\mathfrak h}$ is affine in a projector, so three distinct eigenvalues exclude **every** $\mathfrak h$ at once, which also makes the third row's appeal to the family's value at $r=31$ unnecessary, and that appeal was invalid anyway, reading the closed form above the cap [L23](ungraded-slack.md) proves of that very subalgebra.

**And the two reviewers place L58 against [L8](graded-half.md) the same way**: L8's refuted ansatz $(q\Pi_{\mathfrak k}-p\Pi_{\mathfrak m})/\sqrt{pq(p+q)}$ **is** the Casimir shape, so **L58's mechanism is why L8 was downgraded this morning**, and neither block cross-linked to the other.

**Two collateral repairs.** [results.md](../results.md) cited L58 for non-equivariant graded points while both surviving witnesses are equivariant symmetric pairs; and **[conventions.md](conventions.md) had traps W3 and W4 swapped in both directions**, so a reader checking whether the withdrawn witness's screen was trustworthy landed on the wrong trap.

**One of [r547](../reviews/REVIEW_r547.md)'s findings is recorded with a caveat rather than as stated.** It reports [trap W4](INDEX-traps.md) making $S=0$ vacuous at the attaining family, and the trap does name padded $W_{\mathbb{CP}^2}$ with kernel $63$ of $66$ at $n=12$. **But $S=0$ is a *defining condition* of the residual set, not a gradedness certificate in it**, so the point belongs to the set by definition and the supremum is unaffected. What the trap costs is the *reading* of that condition as gradedness, which matters wherever the residual is described in those words. *Recorded that way rather than as a defect in the measurement, since the lesson of three self-corrections today is to check what a condition quantifies over before acting on it.*

**Verdict, both reviewers in: PLAUSIBLE, agreeing.** [r547](../reviews/REVIEW_r547.md) and [r548](../reviews/REVIEW_r548.md), identical briefs. **The measurement is sound; the label, the notation and the corroboration count all overstated it.**

**It bounds the residual from the wrong side, and the block did not say so.** The set-builder was displayed unrestricted with an equality, the catalogue qualifier surviving only in the prose beside it, so what it gives is $\sup_{\mathcal N}\theta\ge\sqrt{3/2}$ where the conjecture needs $\le$. **[results.md](../results.md) already carried the correct reading**, that this is a measurement over a catalogue proving nothing about points outside it; **the findings block was the only statement of L40 in the record that dropped the scope.**

**"Three independent hunts agree" is one hunt, and [r548](../reviews/REVIEW_r548.md) shows why the other two could not have disagreed.** The $n=6$ run is struck 247 lines above in the same file. And the two closure sweeps are **forced by the record's own theorems**, padding preserves $\theta$, and a $\circ$-orthogonal sum has $\lvert\theta\rvert<\min(\lvert\theta_1\rvert,\lvert\theta_2\rvert)$ strictly, so the supremum over the closure equals the supremum over the catalogue by proof. *An agreement that a theorem guarantees is not corroboration.*

**The attainment is a theorem and the margin was not evidence of it.** $\theta^2=3/2$ holds exactly at $W_{\mathbb{CP}^2}$ by closed form; the $-6.7\times10^{-16}$ is about three ulp, certifies a failure to separate rather than an equality, is blind to a violator at $10^{-6}$, and named no run. **The block's macros were report-only, so the one number the Observation exists to report never rendered in the record at all.**

**The report offered attainment as a reason the residual is well posed**, which is the reverse of a reason, attainment would *settle* it, and it is open, the non-diagonal set being open and its intersection with the critical set non-compact. Corrected to state what is actually measured.

**Where L40 is clean, and it matters.** Its use of [L37](target.md) is inside the grant, including at the attainer, where the corollary is silent and dimension-only testing would wrongly call the point diagonal; and **it states the correct reason** (decided by L37 rather than by frame inspection) where the report had stated a false one. **All six citations are scoped correctly**, so the overstatement was confined to the block and the repair is local.

### L34, independent validation of r550, while r549 runs

**The statement is true at both dimensions**, [r550](../reviews/REVIEW_r550.md) deriving the $m=8$ case entirely by hand: padding is genuinely Weyl, spectrum $\{-1/\sqrt3^{\times2},\,1/(2\sqrt3)^{\times4}\}$, unit norm, trace zero, and $\theta=-\sqrt3/2$ from orthogonal supports killing the cross brackets with $[J_1,J_2]=2J_3$ at the pair cap.

**The finding that matters lands on this audit's own [L33](stratum-two-negative.md) repair, and does not break it.** Of the four properties that repair needed of the $m=8$ witness, three hold outright; the third ("no $4$-plane complex structure"), **holds only of the generic eigenvector**. Each $\omega_i$ is a unit self-dual $2$-form on its plane, so $\mathbb R\omega_1$ and $\mathbb R\omega_2$ **are** exactly [L33](stratum-two-negative.md)'s exception, and the witness refutes the distinct-value reading only under the existential reading of "that eigenvector".

**Checked here: the repair stands on its primary witness.** L33's $\mathrm{Weyl}_4$ witness has $W^+=W^-=\operatorname{diag}(2,-1,-1)$, whose $+2$ eigenspace contains a **rank-$2$** element, and a complex structure on a $4$-plane has rank $4$, so that element is not one under any reading. **L34's role there is corroborative, as the block itself says in calling it "a second witness".** *Recorded because a finding that touches a repair made hours earlier is exactly what the audit should surface, and because the containment lesson of today says to check it rather than assume it.*

| two steps are asserted | "the support is the union of the planes" conflates the span of the *negative* eigenvectors with the padding planes, and needs Ricci-flatness, an identity this file uses 96 lines above; and "each summand contributes exactly one negative eigenvalue" gives only $n_-\le2$ where planes overlap |
