**Verdict, both reviewers in: CONFIRMED.** [r549](../reviews/REVIEW_r549.md) and [r550](../reviews/REVIEW_r550.md), identical briefs, each rebuilding both witnesses from the definitions and each recovering $\operatorname{tr}W^\#=-1$ at both. **The label is earned** (the proof is a construction with closed-form spectra and no computation) and this round is the Gate 2 the row lacked.

**The reviewers divide only on how the $m=8$ witness bears on [L33](stratum-two-negative.md), and both readings are right of different things.** With multiplicity the witness has a two-dimensional negative eigenspace and sits **outside** L33's hypothesis; under the distinct-value reading it sits inside, and there each summand $\omega_i$ **is** L33's exception while the generic element of the eigenspace is not. **So the witness bears on L33 only under the existential reading of "that eigenvector"**, which is precisely the ambiguity this audit's L33 repair removed. **That repair rests on its primary $\mathrm{Weyl}_4$ witness, whose $+2$ eigenspace contains a rank-$2$ element**, and a $4$-plane complex structure has rank $4$, so it is not one under any reading. **The repair stands and L34's role there is corroborative, as the block itself says.**

**And the repair had not reached its quoted copies.** Three sites, twice in [results.md](../results.md) and once in the report, still read "exactly one eigenvalue of one sign", the phrasing L33's own box calls false. **A repair applied to a block and not to the sentences quoting it is a repair the record does not have**, which is the second time today that shape has appeared, after the findings filed by earlier reviewers and never folded in. All three corrected.

**Four further repairs.** The provenance is **half true**, the first clause survives against git, the only one in this file to do so, while the second does not, [results.md](../results.md) still carrying the construction byte-identical and [isotropy-cap.md](isotropy-cap.md) holding an unlinked copy of the whole block. A closing sentence calling the stratum bound "now unproved" is superseded by [Lemma L35](stratum-two-negative.md) below it. A measured figure conflicting with [isotropy-cap.md](isotropy-cap.md)'s value for the same family is marked as measured and unresolved. **And the block's reason for silence above $m=8$ is strengthened**: a summand with one negative eigenvalue has a one-dimensional negative eigenspace, which [L33](stratum-two-negative.md) forces onto a $4$-plane, so $\dim U\le8$ is a fact about the whole family rather than a limitation of two particular planes.

### Lemma L28, the $\mathbb{CP}^m$ family is nondegenerate at every $m\ge3$

**Verdict, both reviewers in: the Statement is correct at every $m\ge3$, and the proof carried one arithmetic error that is inert for the conclusion.** [r551](../reviews/REVIEW_r551.md) CONFIRMED and [r552](../reviews/REVIEW_r552.md) REPAIRED, identical briefs. Each rebuilt the $U(m)$ branching by a route sharing nothing with the block's, one from $S^2(\Lambda^2V)\ominus S^2_0\ominus\mathbb R\ominus\Lambda^4$ by Cauchy and Littlewood–Richardson, the other from the stable rule with $m=3$ filled by $SU(3)$ tensor products, and every dimension, $\theta^2$, the orbit dimension, $\dim E(\tfrac12)$ and $\lambda_{\max}$ reproduced.

**The error: the charge list named a summand that does not exist.** $c_S+q_S^2=4m$ is a Casimir tabulated without its charge added. The true list carries $4(m+1)$ **twice**, on $V_{(2,1);(1)}\oplus\text{conj}$ and on $V_{(2);(2)}$, both at $L_e=0$, which is what the block's own *Argument* paragraph said, so the block contradicted itself and the wrong half was the table. **Refuted four ways**: a Casimir computation, [the record's own Howe–Tan–Willenbring notes](../references/howe-tan-willenbring-2005-stable-branching/notes.md), which carry the correct seven-row table; the trace identity $\operatorname{tr}L_e=0$; and Fox's second moment $\operatorname{tr}(L_e^2)=\kappa_F(2m)\lVert e\rVert^2$. **It moves no eigenvalue to $\tfrac12$**, since $\frac1{m+1}=\frac12$ has no solution at $m\ge3$, so the conclusion never depended on it.

**And it had propagated.** [second-order.md](second-order.md) carried the identical defective list, beside a table of its own that already gave $0$ on both classes, so that file contradicted itself too. Corrected in both, with the trace argument stated beside the table.

**A block headed "canonical statement and proof" was the weaker of two copies.** [second-order.md](second-order.md) holds the fuller treatment, the complete table, explicit $c_S$ and $q_S$, the $\sharp$ closed forms, and the caveat that $\sharp(P_{\mathfrak u},\cdot)$ loses $28$–$46\%$ of its image out of $\mathrm{Weyl}_n$, and the canonical block never cited it. **A canonical block that does not know about the better copy in the next file is canonical only in name.**

## Two things the audit adds, and they are the reason it was worth running

**Multiplicity-freeness is now a theorem at every $m\ge3$.** The block cited [Howe–Tan–Willenbring](https://arxiv.org/abs/math/0311159), whose stable rule needs $\lfloor m/2\rfloor\ge2$, that is $m\ge4$, and **$m=3$ is exactly the case the argument turns on**, being where $\frac2{m+1}=\frac12$ and where the coincidence has to be disposed of. A proof citing a rule out of range at its own crux is citing nothing there. The gap closes by hand: at $m=3$ every mixed piece is a standard $\mathfrak{su}(3)$ tensor product, and $\bar{\mathbf3}\otimes\mathbf3=\mathbf1\oplus\mathbf8$ produces **no** $V_{(1,1);(1,1)}$, which is why the coincidence is vacuous. **The summand is absent, not zero-dimensional**, the formula $\frac{m^2(m+1)(m-3)}4$ vanishing there is the analytic continuation agreeing with the absence rather than the reason for it.

**$\lambda_{\max}$ is now derived from the trace identity rather than from the charge formula.** Only two of the seven values are structural, $\lambda=1$ on $\langle W\rangle$ from $Q(e)=e$, and $\tfrac12$ on the orbit from equivariance alone. The other five were computed, and the block's three independent attestations were spent on the orbit's $\tfrac12$, **the one row that needed none.** The row carrying "not a maximiser" had the charge formula alone behind it, the very formula the error was in. Solving $\operatorname{tr}L_e=0$ for the unknown on the $\mathfrak{su}(m)$ adjoint gives $(m^2-1)y=\frac{(m+2)(m-1)}2$, hence $y=\frac{m+2}{2(m+1)}$, and the same solve returns each remaining value uniquely from the other four.

**Three further repairs.** The block asserted criticality and $\theta^2$ without saying they are proved elsewhere, criticality from $\dim\mathrm{Weyl}_{2m}^{U(m)}=1$ at [target.md](target.md), $\theta^2$ from Fox's Lemma 10.1 at [algebra.md](algebra.md); both now cited, and $\lVert e\rVert^2=1/\theta^2$ recorded as the check that catches the projector coefficients and the closed form together. The $\mathbb{CP}^3$ coincidence at $\Theta_6$ is now explained where it appears, rather than left to read as a problem: $\Theta_n$ is the **diagonal** maximum, padded $W_{\mathbb{CP}^2}$ already puts $\sqrt{3/2}$ into $\mathcal W_6$, and the other point at that value is [L27](nondegeneracy.md)'s excluded $(3,3)$, which fails maximality at cubic order, so both points at $\theta=\sqrt{10/9}$ fail, for different reasons. And [target.md](target.md) evaluated L28's closed form at $m=2$, outside its $m\ge3$ hypothesis; the citation now goes to [algebra.md](algebra.md), whose Fox derivation covers every even $r$ from $r=2$.

**The reviewers split on that last citation and the split is resolved against one of them.** [r551](../reviews/REVIEW_r551.md) proposed [L10](graded-half.md); [r552](../reviews/REVIEW_r552.md) proposed [algebra.md](algebra.md). **L10 states that the reducible-isotropy families sit below the threshold, which is a different claim**, and $m=2$ sits *at* $\sqrt{3/2}$ rather than below it. [algebra.md](algebra.md) states the closed form, its monotonicity in $r$, and the maximum at $r=4$, which is precisely what the citing line asserts.

**Owed.** The affine dependence of $\lambda_S$ on $c_S+q_S^2$ is asserted, not derived; given it, the two constants are forced by $L_ee=e$ and the orbit's $\tfrac12$, and the seven values then pass two moment identities, but affineness itself has no proof on the record. The $\sharp$ closed forms behind it are exact in $\mathrm{GF}(p)$ at $m=3\ldots20$ rather than symbolic. Both are now flagged in the block's "What is not proved", which previously conceded only the multiplicity-freeness citation that this audit discharged.

## A candidate for the canon, raised by this result and by the two before it

$\operatorname{tr}L_W=0$ identically on $\mathcal W_n$ is unnumbered, sitting as a remark in [second-order.md](second-order.md) with its proof in [r529](../reviews/REVIEW_r529.md), $\operatorname{tr}L_e=\langle e,\sum_iQ(V_i)\rangle$, with $\sum_iQ(V_i)$ basis-independent by $O(n)$-equivariance of $Q$. **It has now checked three isotypic tables and repaired two**: it forced the $-\mu$ on $S^2_0\otimes S^2_0$ in [L27](nondegeneracy.md), it sums to zero off [L127](second-order.md)'s sign-character table, and here it both discriminates the block's two paragraphs and derives the row the conclusion rests on. **A fact that checks every isotypic decomposition of $L_e$ the programme produces belongs in the numbered canon**, where the next brief can reach it.

### Lemma L66, no maximizer is $\circ$-decomposable

**Verdict, both reviewers in: the theorem is true and its proof is sound. Five sentences around it were false.** [r553](../reviews/REVIEW_r553.md) and [r554](../reviews/REVIEW_r554.md), identical briefs, both CONFIRMED. At 34 mentions this was the most-cited unaudited result on the record, and it is a leaf.

**The proof rests on two facts it never states, and both are now in it.** The additivity step (*"$A_i\circ A_j=0$ makes $P$ additive"*), needs the **total** symmetry of $T(X,Y,Z)=\langle X,Y\circ Z\rangle$: expanding, the cross term is $\langle A_i,Q(A_j)\rangle=T(A_i,A_j,A_j)$, and it dies only after moving the first slot to $\langle A_j,A_i\circ A_j\rangle=0$. Symmetry in the last two slots is free from the polarisation; symmetry in the first is equivalent to $\nabla P=3Q$. Both reviewers derived it, one term of $Q$ at a time, $\tfrac14(\operatorname{tr}(XYZ)+\operatorname{tr}(XZY))$ for the square, and for the $\sharp$ part a raw tensor identity in which each transposition flips both totally antisymmetric structure constants. **The record held the fact all along**, at [second-order.md](second-order.md), and [support-dichotomy.md](support-dichotomy.md) cited it nowhere.

**And the closing strict inequality needs $M(n)>0$**, which nothing said. $M(n)\ge\sqrt{3/2}$ at every $n\ge4$ from padded $W_{\mathbb{CP}^2}$, so the fact is cheap, but the lemma's entire content is the strictness, and it rested on an unstated positivity.

**$M(n)$ was defined nowhere on the record.** It is used in six files and in the report; [conventions.md](conventions.md) had no row for it and already warns that the letter $M$ carries two other objects; its only written definition sat inside a review. **A symbol carrying the global maximum, load-bearing in the proof of the most-cited unaudited result, undefined in the file that exists to define exactly this.** It now has a row.

## The five false sentences

**The canonical proof in [results.md](../results.md) was invalid.** It closes $\lvert P(W)\rvert\le\sum_i\lvert a_i\rvert^3\lvert P(A_i)\rvert<\max_i\lvert P(A_i)\rvert$, *"so one summand alone does better"*, which reads $0<0$ at a splitting whose every piece has $P(A_i)=0$, constructible at $n=8$ by the intermediate value theorem on two disjoint blocks, and where no summand does better. **The conclusion survives**, the bound running against $M(n)$ instead.

**The $\min_i\lvert\theta_i\rvert$ clause is refuted.** [results.md](../results.md) attached *"the value of such a sum is strictly below $\min_i\lvert\theta_i\rvert$"*. It is the harmonic-square law $\frac1{\theta^2}=\sum_i\frac1{\theta_i^2}$ from [nondegeneracy.md](nondegeneracy.md), **which holds at a critical point**, and criticality is what L66 discards three lines earlier, in bold, as the source of its whole reach. Both reviewers built witnesses; the reason needs none. Additivity gives $P(W)=\sum_ia_i^3\theta_i$, so sending one coefficient to $1$ sends $P(W)$ to that piece's own $\theta$: **the clause fails on an open set of coefficient vectors whenever the pieces differ in $\lvert\theta\rvert$**, and criticality is exactly what removes that freedom by pinning the coefficients. Restated with its hypothesis and cited to the law.

**[INDEX.md](INDEX.md) printed the chain without the absolute values.** At $(a,b)=(0.6,-0.8)$ with pieces at $\pm M$, $\sum_ia_i^3P(A_i)=0.728M>0.6M=M(n)\max_ia_i$, so the printed second inequality is false, and [support-dichotomy.md](support-dichotomy.md) records that counterexample in its own text. **The index is where a reader meets this result first, and it carried the version the record had already withdrawn.**

**The direct-sum saddle lemma is incomparable, not stronger, and it is in the wrong file.** [support-dichotomy.md](support-dichotomy.md) said [nondegeneracy.md](nondegeneracy.md) *"states it verbatim and stronger"*. It is in [ATTEMPTS.md](../ATTEMPTS.md), unnumbered; nondegeneracy.md only mentions it as being on record; and its hypothesis (a split over disjoint coordinate blocks with each piece critical), is **narrower** than $\circ$-orthogonality. So it reaches local maxima where L66 reaches only global ones, and L66 reaches non-critical pieces and splittings sharing coordinates where it reaches none. Neither implies the other.

**$\Theta_4$ was used for the global maximum**, in *"$\Theta_4=\sqrt{3/2}$ against $\theta_0(4)=0.866$"*. By [conventions.md](conventions.md) $\Theta_4=\theta_0(4)=0.866$, so the sentence as written compares $0.866$ to itself. The quantity meant is $M(4)$. **The retraction the sentence records survives; the symbol does not.**

**And the $\dim E(1)=1$ route is not a second proof of L66.** [second-order.md](second-order.md) called it one. With $e=W/\theta$ and $\circ$-orthogonality, $e\circ A_j=\frac{a_j}\theta Q(A_j)$, which equals $A_j$ only when $Q(A_j)\propto A_j$, so the route needs the pieces **critical** and is strictly weaker. Scoped.

## What this says about the record rather than about the lemma

**Every repair applied here had been filed before and never reached the copies.** The total-symmetry gap by [r281](../reviews/REVIEW_r281.md) and twice by [r283](../reviews/REVIEW_r283.md); the absolute values by [r281](../reviews/REVIEW_r281.md) and [r282](../reviews/REVIEW_r282.md). *That is the same shape as [L33](stratum-two-negative.md)'s repair failing to reach three quoted copies, found hours earlier.* **A finding filed and not applied is a finding the record does not have**, and this result had three of them sitting live while being cited 34 times.

**The 34 citations are clean on the hypothesis**, no site uses L66 against local maxima or with merely Frobenius-orthogonal pieces. One figure node tagged a support-and-padding clause to L66 alone, which the lemma explicitly disclaims; both figures now name [L30](stratum-two-negative.md) beside it.

**A new warning, W5, in [INDEX-traps.md](INDEX-traps.md).** $\Theta_n$ is the diagonal maximum and two consecutive audits found it read as the global one, here, and at [L28](nondegeneracy.md) where $\mathbb{CP}^3$ sitting at $\Theta_6$ read as a contradiction with [L24](../results.md). **The two share a cause**: the file wanting the global maximum had no defined symbol for it.

**Owed.** [r553](../reviews/REVIEW_r553.md) shows the saddle lemma generalises off coordinate blocks, restricting $P$ to the circle through a $\circ$-orthogonal pair gives $f''=3\theta$ at the interior critical point, using only $\circ$-orthogonality. **So L66 and [ATTEMPTS.md](../ATTEMPTS.md)'s Theorem A can be joined into one numbered family instead of three unlinked copies**, with the local-maximum conclusion recovered under $\circ$-orthogonality alone. That derivation is not yet on the record.

### Lemma L94, $\kappa^2\le2\lVert M\rVert_F^2$, equality exactly at coordinate-diagonal, a Gate 2 the record had and lost

**Recovered while auditing [L54](bispectral.md), whose rigidity clause is exactly this equality case.** [r101](../reviews/REVIEW_r101.md) and [r102](../reviews/REVIEW_r102.md) ran identical briefs of five claims; **claim 2 is L94's statement verbatim**, and both returned **CONFIRMED with a one-line proof** of the bound and of the "iff". That is a Gate 2 by this programme's own definition.

**Three indexes carried none of it.** [INDEX-results.md](INDEX-results.md) said "no verdict on the record"; this ledger held no entry; and the two rows in [reviews/INDEX.md](../reviews/INDEX.md) read `Verdicts | CONFIRMED/REFUTED` and named no subject, so nothing connected those reviewers to the lemma. **The block itself said "Gate 2 landed, Reviewers 101 and 102, agreeing" the whole time**, and no index believed it.

**What it cost.** L54 was selected as a leaf because its block names $t\le2$ without the number, so a dependency scan reading identifiers saw no edge; had the number been there, the scan would have found an input the index called unaudited and the ordering would have stalled on a verdict that already existed. **Two independent failures of the same kind (a statement recorded without its identifier), pointing in opposite directions and cancelling.**

**The repair is bookkeeping and it is recorded as such.** No mathematics was re-derived here: the verdict row, the ledger entry and the two reviewer rows now say what [r101](../reviews/REVIEW_r101.md) and [r102](../reviews/REVIEW_r102.md) said. **A verdict that exists in two reports and in no index is a verdict the next brief cannot find**, which is the whole reason the index carries verdicts at all.

### Lemma L54, the two diagonals, and rigidity at $t=2$

**Verdict, both reviewers in: the mathematics L54 reaches for is correct, and the numbered block states it in a basis where it is false.** [r555](../reviews/REVIEW_r555.md) and [r556](../reviews/REVIEW_r556.md), identical briefs, both REFUTED-as-written and REPAIRED by one clause.

**The refutation.** $\sum d_\psi^2=t$ holds only when the $\psi$'s diagonalise $K$ on $\mathbf1^\perp$; for any other orthonormal basis the sum is strictly smaller, by the same strict convexity that drives the rigidity. **NPW's $\psi_k$ is an explicit staircase basis fixed by the frame alone, carrying no dependence on $W$**, so it is never a $K$-eigenbasis except by accident, and *a hollow nonzero matrix is never diagonal in its own frame*, which makes the failure guaranteed rather than generic. Hand witness at $n=4$, coordinate-diagonal, $t=2$ (**the exact configuration the rigidity clause is about**), gives $\sum d^2=\tfrac32$ against the claimed $3$, so the premise "equal norms on both sides" fails outright.

**The section 190 lines above says "taking the $\psi$'s as $K$'s eigenvectors" twice.** The block dropped that hypothesis and substituted a named basis violating it, and the substitution propagated verbatim to [results.md](../results.md) and to the report. **A block that restates a section's result in the section's own file, and loses one of its hypotheses on the way, is the third numbered block this audit has found whose argument lives elsewhere**, after [L66](support-dichotomy.md) and [L34](stratum-two-negative.md).

**Three identities are derived here that the record presented as measured.** The $\varphi$-diagonal of $\mathring W$ and of $M$ equalling $K$ is three lines from Li's $iklj$ order plus pair antisymmetry; $\Pi_{\mathrm{diag}}\mathring W\Pi_{\mathrm{diag}}=-K\vert_{\mathbf1^\perp}$ follows with $K\mathbf1=0$ as trace-freeness. The script citations are corroboration, and the block read as though they were the grounds.

**The rigidity's stronger reading is true and the block's route does not reach it.** Schur--Horn with equal sums and equal norms gives $\mu^\downarrow=d_\varphi^\downarrow$, **the sorted vectors**. Equality of the vectors themselves additionally asserts $\mathring W$ is diagonal in that basis, and it does hold at $t_F=2$, by the Cauchy--Schwarz equality that makes $M$ diagonal in the $\varphi$-basis. **So the block asserts more than its stated argument delivers, and the cheaper argument was available.**

## The finding worth more than the block: the odd-$n$ shortfall is not permanent

**The section's headline ("its reach is EVEN $n$, and the shortfall at odd $n$ is permanent"), is REFUTED.**

**The cap's equality case forces $n$ even.** The only shape attaining $\mu_1=\sqrt{t(n-2)/(n-1)}$ is $K=\alpha uu^{\mathsf T}+\beta(\Pi_{\mathbf1^\perp}-uu^{\mathsf T})$, and hollowness $K_{aa}=0$ makes $u_a^2$ constant, so $u=\varepsilon/\sqrt n$ with $\varepsilon_a=\pm1$; $u\perp\mathbf1$ then forces $\sum_a\varepsilon_a=0$, **impossible at odd $n$.** So the bound is strictly slack at every odd $n$, **a property of the cap, which the section read as a property of $\Theta_n$.**

**The lossy step is that the eigenvalue-spread argument discards hollowness**, spending only $K\mathbf1=0$ and $\lVert K\rVert_F^2=t$. Replacing it with one Cauchy--Schwarz against the vertex space $\mathcal D_n$ gives a closed form in $\sum_av_a^4$ that reproduces the cap exactly at even $n$ and yields $\tfrac{n-1}n\mu\le\Theta_n$ at odd $n$.

**The file's own number already named the target.** Its printed shortfall ratio $\frac{n^3-3n^2+4}{n^3-3n^2}$ factors as $\frac{(n-2)^2(n+1)}{n^2(n-3)}$, since $(n^2-4n+4)(n+1)=n^3-3n^2+4$ exactly, which is the square of the factor by which the cap overshoots $\tfrac n{n-1}\Theta_n$. **The record computed the gap correctly, in closed form, and read it as a wall.**

**And the document the section cites already proves it.** The external report (unpublished, held in the private record) carries a parity-sensitive fourth-moment lemma covering both parities, and its $\lvert Z(h)\rvert^2$ is half $\lVert P_{\mathcal D_n}(vv^{\mathsf T})\rVert_F^2$ identically, **two routes built independently onto one function.** So the record was strictly weaker at odd $n$ than the source it was citing.

**A consumer was outside the record's hypotheses.** [target.md](target.md) runs the dichotomy on $\tfrac{n-1}n\mu\le\Theta_n$, which [bispectral.md](bispectral.md) identified with its own $\theta_0(n)$ cap, sharper at odd $n$, and the two coincide only at even $n$. The over-claim is scoped and the consumer joined to both halves.

## Smaller repairs, and one correction to a source

The frame quantifier substituted $\max_Ft_F$ into a frame-wise argument and now reads $t_F=2$ *in that frame*. The parity sentence attached "at EVEN $n$ only" to an identity that holds at every $n$ ($\sqrt{2(n-2)/(n-1)}=n\theta_0(n)/(n-1)$ needs no parity condition) where it belongs to the relevance. [conventions.md](conventions.md) defined none of $d$, $d_\varphi$, $d_\psi$, $t$ or $\kappa$, and $d$ meant two different things in one file; all now have rows, with the per-frame and extremal readings of $t$ distinguished, **which is the second undefined load-bearing symbol this audit has found in two results**, after $M(n)$.

**NPW Proposition 3.10 is transcribed with the paper's own typo**, $-k$ where $-(n-k)$ belongs. As printed the tensor is trace-free only at $k=n/2$; the printed normaliser $(n-k+1)(n-k)$ is exactly $(n-k)^2+(n-k)$, which the $-k$ form does not produce. Corrected in the notes, with the reason. Nothing downstream uses the explicit form.

### Lemma L44, $n\ge12$ is necessary, not merely sufficient

**Verdict, both reviewers in: the mathematics is right and the witness was placed on a boundary it is not on.** [r557](../reviews/REVIEW_r557.md) REPAIRED and [r558](../reviews/REVIEW_r558.md) CONFIRMED, identical briefs, each rebuilding $\theta(W_{\mathbb{CP}^2})=\sqrt{3/2}$ by hand from the $\mathfrak{so}(4)$ structure constants, $[\omega_1,\omega_2]=-\sqrt2\,\omega_3$ gives $c_{123}^2=2$, hence $W^\#=2(w_2w_3,w_1w_3,w_1w_2)$ and $Q(W)=\sqrt{3/2}\,W$ exactly, and each verifying every attribution verbatim against the stored sources.

**The false sentence.** *"The zero-padded $W_{\mathbb{CP}^2}$ ... lies on $\partial\Omega_0$."* **A Weyl tensor has $\mathrm{ric}=0$, which is exactly where $F=\lVert R\rVert^2/\lVert\mathrm{ric}(R)\rVert^2$ blows up and $\Omega_0$ is undefined**, so the tensor is outside the closed cone rather than on its boundary. The boundary point is $cI+W$ at the pinned $c$. **The record already said so in three places**: [results.md](../results.md) states it correctly, and [problem.md](problem.md) itself, 285 lines below the block, says *"Ricci-flatness would additionally need $c=0$, and there $\partial\Omega_0$ forces $\lVert W\rVert=0$"*, which refutes the block's sentence from a route sharing nothing with the reviewers' $\mathrm{scal}=0$ argument. **Three sites wrong and one right; all four now agree.** [r90](../reviews/REVIEW_r90.md) had already corrected this exact sentence-shape elsewhere.

**The parity worry is refuted, and the refutation is worth recording.** I had derived that $\Theta_n^2<\tfrac32$ gives $n^2-13n+18<0$ rather than the block's $n^2-12n+8<0$, and put it to both reviewers as the leading question. **Both refuted it as an attack on this block**: the cone criterion's constant is $c(n-1)=\theta_0(n)$, forced by $\lVert I\rVert^2=\binom n2$ and the boundary equation, a rational function of $n$ with no parity anywhere. **$\Theta_n$ is what the diagonal locus *attains*; it is never what the criterion *demands*.** The parity polynomial is real and belongs to the *elementary* reading, where [Lemma L2](../results.md) already carries both roots, and both give the same integer threshold, $6+2\sqrt7\approx11.29$ against $\tfrac{13+\sqrt{97}}2\approx11.42$.

**The conclusion is overdetermined and the block proves the weaker half the long way.** Padded $W_{\mathbb{CP}^2}$ sits at $\sqrt{3/2}$ at every $n\ge4$ while the sphere-product maximum is $\Theta_n$, so wherever $\Theta_n<\sqrt{3/2}$ the conjecture's conclusion is **false outright**, witness in hand and no cone theory involved. *The cone version still earns its keep*, against [Beitz](../references/beitz-2018-bianchi-convexity/notes.md)'s own conjecture, which states $n\ge12$ without deriving it, and for the Ricci-flow statement that $\Omega_0$ is non-invariant there, neither of which the elementary witness gives. **Both routes are now stated.**

**Three more repairs.** The block never cited [Lemma L43](../results.md), which supplies its only step. It uses *invariance $\Rightarrow G\le0$*, where Beitz's Lemma 2.3.4 gives only the converse, the direction holds in two lines and is now stated rather than assumed. And the heading's "not merely sufficient" presupposes sufficiency, which is the open conjecture; what is proved is that $n\ge12$ cannot be weakened, and [results.md](../results.md) carries that guard where the block omitted it.

**A novelty claim the record had already withdrawn twice.** *"The witness and the conclusion drawn from it are new here"*, the witness is Nienhaus's, credited so at [problem.md](problem.md)'s own reviewer-90 note and in the report. Only the conclusion is new. **This is the seventh finding today that was filed once and never applied.**

**And a citation uncheckable from the notes.** Xu's (3.1)–(3.4) verify verbatim against the stored full text, and [the Xu notes](../references/xu-2024-ricci-flow-invariant-conditions/notes.md) carry none of those equations, so a reader following the record's own reading cannot check that attribution. Recorded in the block.

## A standing finding of this audit: a repair filed once is not a repair the record has

**Seven times in one day, the audit found a defect whose correction was already on the record and had never reached the text it corrects.** The instances are unrelated mathematically and identical in shape, which is what makes it a property of the record rather than a run of bad luck.

| where | what was already filed | what still stood |
|---|---|---|
| [L33](stratum-two-negative.md) | the repaired phrasing, in the block itself | three quoted copies, in [results.md](../results.md) twice and in the report, carrying the phrasing the repaired block calls false |
| [L66](support-dichotomy.md) | the additivity step's reliance on the total symmetry of $T$, filed by [r281](../reviews/REVIEW_r281.md) and twice by [r283](../reviews/REVIEW_r283.md) | the file cited the fact nowhere, though the record proves it twice in [second-order.md](second-order.md) |
| [L66](support-dichotomy.md) | the absolute values as load-bearing, filed by [r281](../reviews/REVIEW_r281.md) and [r282](../reviews/REVIEW_r282.md), with a counterexample the file still prints | [INDEX.md](INDEX.md) carried the withdrawn absolute-value-free chain, where a reader meets the result first |
| [L94](bispectral.md) | a Gate 2, in [r101](../reviews/REVIEW_r101.md) and [r102](../reviews/REVIEW_r102.md), both CONFIRMED with a one-line proof | three indexes recording no verdict, and two reviewer rows naming no subject |
| [L16](problem.md) | *"THE REPAIR IS FREE AND IS OWED"*, with the parity-correct crossover derived and its cost priced | the bound still stated against $\theta_0(n)$, the block recording the repair rather than performing it |
| [L44](problem.md) | the boundary sentence-shape, corrected by [r90](../reviews/REVIEW_r90.md) elsewhere | three sites placing a Weyl tensor on $\partial\Omega_0$, where $F$ blows up |
| [L44](problem.md) | the witness credited to Nienhaus, twice | the block claiming the witness as new |

**Why every step of it is legal.** A reviewer files a finding; the cycle records it in the ledger; the ledger is the right place for it; the cycle closes with the record consistent. **Nothing in that sequence is the moment where applying it was skipped**, which is why rereading the rule does not catch it, the same shape as the `Deliverable:` trailer's problem, and it wants the same kind of answer: a check where the status changes.

**What follows for the audit, concretely.** A finding is closed when the mathematics is right in every place that states it, so **the sweep runs over the statement rather than the identifier**, and a repair applied to a block is unfinished until the blocks quoting it are read. *That rule already stands; what these add is evidence of how often it fails, and where.*

**A sweep of [INDEX-results.md](INDEX-results.md) sharpens it: the index is the last thing updated, and it is the first thing a brief reads.** Of the numbered rows, $69$ carry a Gate 2 verdict, $56$ say explicitly that none is on the record, and **$20$ say neither**, silent about their own status. Five of those twenty are the named subject of a reviewer pair, and **three carried the Gate 2 inside their own block while the row said nothing**: [L74](diagonal-half.md) ([r339](../reviews/REVIEW_r339.md), [r340](../reviews/REVIEW_r340.md)), [L124](second-order.md) ([r365](../reviews/REVIEW_r365.md), [r366](../reviews/REVIEW_r366.md)) and [L125](second-order.md) ([r367](../reviews/REVIEW_r367.md), [r368](../reviews/REVIEW_r368.md)), each of whose blocks already records the reviewers and the withdrawals they forced. With [L94](bispectral.md) that is **four results standing in the queue for a third pair of reviewers they did not need**, and the awaiting count falls from $76$ to $73$. [L70](bounds.md) and [L71](bounds.md) remain candidates: each block names one reviewer of its pair, so they want reading rather than bookkeeping.

**So the failure is narrower and more fixable than \"findings go unapplied\".** The blocks absorbed these repairs correctly and on time. What lagged is the index, and a verdict that exists in a block, in two reports and in no index is a verdict the next brief cannot find, which is the whole reason the index carries verdicts.

### Lemma L51, the variation calculus is an identity along a critical orbit

**Verdict, both reviewers in: CONFIRMED, and the block under-claims its own result.** [r559](../reviews/REVIEW_r559.md) and [r560](../reviews/REVIEW_r560.md), identical briefs, each rederiving both identities, all consequences and the exact jet from the definitions, and each verifying the Krasnov–Tkachev attribution, one from the record's notes, one from its own jet down to the sign of the $-9\theta\lvert c\rvert^{-4}$ term. **The argument is inside the block**, which after three consecutive results whose arguments lived elsewhere is worth saying.

**The guard named the empty set.** *"where $B\succeq0$"* is unsatisfiable as written: $B=\tfrac\theta2\mathrm{Id}-L_W$ and $L_WW=\theta W$, so $BW=-\tfrac\theta2W$ at every $\theta>0$. It must read $B\vert_{W^\perp}\succeq0$, and the Argument's $\Pi$ carries that restriction while the Statement does not. **[r287](../reviews/REVIEW_r287.md), the Gate 2 that produced this block, wrote "on $W^\perp$"; the write-up dropped it, into four copies.** *That is a hypothesis lost in transcription from the review that established it, the same shape as [L54](bispectral.md)'s $\psi$-eigenbasis, lost from a section into the block restating it.*

**And the guard is superfluous, which the block did not know.** The equality $\langle b,B^+b\rangle=\theta/2$ follows from three facts and no positivity: $B(\Pi u)=b$; $b\perp\ker B$, which is the fusion rule already proved; and $[t^4]=0$ along the orbit, since $P$ is constant there. **Positivity licenses only the word *supremum*.** So [results.md](../results.md)'s unguarded headline, *"forces the third-order condition, the fusion rule and the fourth-order inequality to hold with equality on the orbit tangent, with no maximality hypothesis"*, was the more accurate of the two copies, and the canonical block the weaker.

**My own leading question resolves against me, twice over.** I put it to both reviewers that $B\succeq0$ on $W^\perp$ **is** what maximality buys through [L24](second-order.md), so that "none of this uses maximality" could cover only the first three conclusions. **Both refuted it**: the guard is strictly weaker than maximality, with a hand witness, $P(x,y)=x^3+\tfrac32xy^2+\alpha y^3$ at $(1,0)$ has $B\vert_{W^\perp}=0$ and is no local maximum at $\alpha\ne0$, and the clause it guards needs no guard at all.

**A gain the block's own number yields.** Along the chordal curve through an orbit direction the second- and third-order coefficients vanish unconditionally, leaving $-\tfrac38\theta\lVert z\rVert^4$; a local maximum needs that $\le0$, so **$\theta\ge0$ at every local maximum**, the orbit tangent never vanishing because $\mathcal W_n$ carries no $O(n)$-invariant vector. **[L24](second-order.md)'s "local maximiser with $\theta<0$" branch therefore describes an empty set**, and its Scope now says so.

**Three smaller repairs and one refutation next door.** The stationary corrector is $\Pi u$ and not $u$, since $\langle u,W\rangle=-\lVert z\rVert^2$ puts the orbit acceleration outside $W^\perp$. What the block calls the *geodesic* quartic is the **chordal** one, the great-circle coefficient is $\tfrac78\theta-\tfrac52\langle L_Wz,z\rangle$, differing by $2\langle Bz,z\rangle$ and agreeing on the null cone, which is the only place either is evaluated, so no number moves. The truncation of the curve at second order was justified by a cancellation that does not happen; the $t^3$ term contributes $-6\langle Bz,v\rangle$ and vanishes because the orbit velocity lies in $\ker B$. And **[second-order.md](second-order.md) printed one orbit dimension, $18$, for $n=8,10,12$**: the stabiliser of padded $W_{\mathbb{CP}^2}$ is $U(2)\times SO(n-4)$, so the orbit is $\binom n2-4-\binom{n-4}2=18,26,34$, a formula that returns the record's own measured $10$ at $n=6$.

**A scope claim that held at three of four points.** *"Excess $=0$ is Morse–Bott nondegeneracy, already a theorem at the points where it was measured"* covers the three sphere products by [L27](nondegeneracy.md), whose $p,q\ge3$ with $(p,q)\ne(3,3)$ admits $(4,4)$, $(6,6)$ and $(5,7)$, **and not padded $W_{\mathbb{CP}^2}$**, where [L28](nondegeneracy.md) is stated at $m\ge3$ in native dimension and the support is a measurement stopping at $n=10$.

**And the fourth undefined load-bearing symbol set in five results.** $\Pi$, $b$ and $B^+$ carry the whole fourth-order clause and none was defined anywhere on the record; worse, [second-order.md](second-order.md) uses $b$ for a *scalar* forty-four lines above its vector use. With $B$ itself, that is a third object under one letter. All now have rows in [conventions.md](conventions.md), after $M(n)$ at [L66](support-dichotomy.md) and the $d$, $t$, $\kappa$ family at [L54](bispectral.md).

### Lemma L99, a diagonal critical point with two components carrying an edge fails second-order maximality

**Verdict, both reviewers in: the heading is exactly the right statement, it is now proved, and the body stated the withdrawn version twice in bold.** [r561](../reviews/REVIEW_r561.md) and [r562](../reviews/REVIEW_r562.md), identical briefs, both REPAIRED, each proving every one of the block's four numerical claims by hand.

**Every figure in the core was superfluous.** The block certified its claims with $10^{-17}$, $10^{-15}$, a census of some $400$ exact critical points, a $7\times10^{-3}$ residual and a cosine of $1.000000000$. Each measures something that is two or three lines from the definitions:

- **The kernel.** Criticality on the diagonal locus is $M^{\circ2}+M^2=\theta M+\operatorname{diag}(s)$, so $M^2-\theta M=\operatorname{diag}(s)-M^{\circ2}$, **the Laplacian of the weighted graph $w_{ab}=M_{ab}^2$**, its off-diagonal $-M_{ab}^2$ and its diagonal the matching row sum, $M$ being hollow. Its kernel is the component-indicator span; each indicator is in $\ker M$ by within-block Ricci-flatness, and $\ker M\subseteq\ker(M^2-\theta M)$ closes it. **That is [Lemma L129](diagonal-half.md) minus one line, $290$ lines below the block in the same file.**
- **The scaling and value laws.** Block $\circ$-orthogonality gives $Q(M)=\bigoplus\lambda_i^2\theta_iM_i$, so criticality forces $\lambda_i\theta_i=\theta$ and unit norm gives $\theta^2\sum_i\theta_i^{-2}=1$, the harmonic-square law, which [nondegeneracy.md](nondegeneracy.md) and [target.md](target.md) already carry and whose derivation is written out in [plan-diagfam.md](../reviews/plan-diagfam.md), never folded back.

**The refutation, and the repair was already in the file.** The body claimed the failure at *every disconnected* diagonal critical point. **Twelve lines above the heading the same file records the repair** (*"at least two components carrying an edge", not "disconnected"*) and the body kept the old sentence twice, in bold. The file's own padded $S^4\times S^4$ at $n=9$ settles it: its extra components are singletons, $\lambda_{\max}(L_e)=0.5714<1$, contradicting the wider claim outright. **[plan-maximizer.md](../reviews/plan-maximizer.md) had filed this and it was never applied.**

**What replaces it, with sharpness.** For $N_{ab}=a_{c(a)}M_{ab}$, $\langle N,M\rangle=\theta^2\sum_ca_c\theta_c^{-2}$ **over edge-carrying components alone**, a singleton contributing nothing whatever its $a_c$. Writing $k^+$ for their number: at $k^+\ge2$, taking $a_{c_1}=\theta_{c_1}^2$ and $a_{c_2}=-\theta_{c_2}^2$ gives a non-radial admissible $N$ with $\mathcal LN=\theta N$, so $\lambda_{\max}(L_e\vert_{W^\perp})\ge1$ and [L24](second-order.md) fails; at $k^+=1$ the condition forces $N\propto M$; at $k^+=0$ the family is empty. **The admissible eigenspace has dimension $k^+-1$**, so three or more edge-carrying components give a violation of multiplicity at least two, stronger than the single direction the block claimed.

**The mechanism in one sentence, from [r562](../reviews/REVIEW_r562.md): $\theta$ is always an eigenvalue on the radial line, and disconnection raises its multiplicity to $k^+$.** That is why an edgeless component contributes nothing, and it is the whole content of the hypothesis.

**The stated admissibility was the withdrawn one.** *"$a$ constant on each component with $\sum a=0$"*, checked by row sums at $6\times10^{-17}$, both retracted ten and twelve lines above, and the check has no content since row sums vanish identically for this family. The condition is $\sum_ca_c\theta_c^{-2}=0$ over edge-carrying components.

## The conclusion is a fourth copy, and the join is now owed by three results

**[ATTEMPTS.md](../ATTEMPTS.md) already ruled this claim *"on the record four times over"***, the value formula in [target.md](target.md), the consequence as a Theorem in [support-dichotomy.md](support-dichotomy.md), and the same eigenvector $H=\lVert W_2\rVert^2W_1-\lVert W_1\rVert^2W_2$ in its own direct-sum saddle lemma, with the *stronger* conclusion $\mathrm{Hess}=+3\theta\lVert H\rVert^2$. **The archaeology was moved to ATTEMPTS.md and the numbered block was left standing, labelled `Lemma` and cited 21 times.**

**That saddle lemma is the same unnumbered theorem [L66](support-dichotomy.md)'s audit found misattributed this morning**, where [r553](../reviews/REVIEW_r553.md) recommended joining it with L66 into one numbered family. **It now has a third claimant**, and it carries the same padding defect, at $W_2=0$ the eigenvector vanishes, which is exactly the $k^+=1$ case. *One numbered result should replace four unlinked copies plus an archaeology note.*

**Three peripheral rows are Observation-grade and orphaned.** The algebraic candidates' projections, the $n=8$ point with $\Delta=11.16$, and the block-graph statistic are measurements at the dimensions run, and their subjects occur nowhere else on the record, **corrections with no referent.** Marked as observations rather than deleted, since a reader meeting them should know what they are.

**And one retraction reached the wrong file.** L99 retracts the isotypic scalarity claim, and [second-order.md](second-order.md) still asserted *"$L_e$ acts as a scalar on each isotypic component"*. **Schur gives a scalar on an irreducible appearing once and an $m\times m$ block where it appears $m$ times**, the distinction [L27](nondegeneracy.md) and [L28](nondegeneracy.md) both turn on. Scoped there.

**The label stands.** `Lemma` is right for the core once the derivations are written in, on the smaller statement, so **no downgrade and no new obligation**. **And a sixth undefined symbol set**: $\mathrm{Aut}(M)$, $H_A$, $D_X$ and $\mathrm{proj}$ carried the peripheral arguments with no definitions anywhere; $\mathrm{proj}$ in particular hides a hypothesis, the identity $\mathrm{proj}(M^{\circ2})=-\mathrm{proj}(M^2)$ failing for the projection onto $\mathcal D_n$ without the $\perp M$ factor.

## The owed join, now well-posed: one numbered result for four copies

**Three audited results this session point at the same unnumbered theorem, and each found a different defect in the pointing.** The theorem is [ATTEMPTS.md](../ATTEMPTS.md)'s *Theorem A (direct-sum saddle lemma)*: *any critical point with $\theta>0$ that splits over disjoint coordinate blocks is a saddle, with $H=\lVert W_2\rVert^2W_1-\lVert W_1\rVert^2W_2$ giving $\mathrm{Hess}=+3\theta\lVert H\rVert^2$ exactly.* **ATTEMPTS.md flags it "single-run agent result, unreviewed."**

| result | what its audit found about the pointing |
|---|---|
| [L66](support-dichotomy.md) | its file called Theorem A *"verbatim and stronger"* and located it in the wrong file; the two are **incomparable**, Theorem A needs disjoint coordinate blocks with per-piece criticality and reaches local maxima, L66 needs $\circ$-orthogonality with no criticality and reaches global ones |
| [L99](diagonal-half.md) | Theorem A carries L99's conclusion **with the same eigenvector and a stronger form**, and [ATTEMPTS.md](../ATTEMPTS.md) had already ruled the claim *"on the record four times over"* while the numbered block stayed |
| [L99](diagonal-half.md) | **Theorem A inherits the same defect L99 was repaired for**: at $W_2=0$ the eigenvector vanishes, which is exactly the $k^+=1$ case where the family is exhausted by the radial direction. *So "splits over disjoint blocks" must become "at least two blocks are nonzero"*, a padding is not a split |

**What the join has to deliver, and it is now a bounded task.** A single numbered result stating the saddle conclusion under the weakest hypothesis that carries it, with the three known strengths as scoped corollaries: **(i)** $\circ$-orthogonality with no criticality, giving the global-maximum exclusion that is [L66](support-dichotomy.md); **(ii)** per-piece criticality over nonzero blocks, giving the local-maximum exclusion with $\mathrm{Hess}=+3\theta\lVert H\rVert^2$; **(iii)** the diagonal case, where the admissible eigenspace has dimension $k^+-1$ and the hypothesis reads *at least two components carrying an edge*, which is [L99](diagonal-half.md). **[r553](../reviews/REVIEW_r553.md) supplies the bridge**: restricting $P$ to the circle through a $\circ$-orthogonal pair gives $f''=3\theta$ at the interior critical point, using $\circ$-orthogonality alone, so (ii)'s conclusion may reach (i)'s hypothesis, which is the question the join turns on.

**This is new mathematics and goes through two reviewers like anything else.** It is recorded here rather than attempted mid-audit, and it is the first item that should be taken once the numbered results are through, because until it is done, a reader meeting any of the four copies cannot tell which hypothesis they are standing on.

### Lemma L107, the $\Pi$-bound $\theta\le(n-1)\lvert\lambda_{\min}\rvert$

**Verdict, both reviewers in: the inequality is CONFIRMED and is the conjecture's target restated spectrally; the statement around it was wrong on five counts.** [r563](../reviews/REVIEW_r563.md) and [r564](../reviews/REVIEW_r564.md), identical briefs, each rebuilding the bound by two routes. This is the result [LEDGER.md](LEDGER.md) itself named as the one to audit first.

**It is tight, and the brief's premise was backwards.** I put it to both reviewers that the bound might be permanently slack, since the per-row family's negative row returns $\theta_0(n)$ exactly. **Both refuted that**: $(n-1)\lvert\lambda_{\min}\rvert=\theta_0(n)$ **identically** at the balanced product, at $n=6$, trace-freeness gives $6h=9m$ and unit norm $22.5m^2=1$, so $5m=1.0540926=\theta_0(6)$, and by the isotropy mechanism equality holds at **every** Einstein $S^p\times S^q$. The equality case, absent from the block, is $\operatorname{supp}(g)\subseteq E_{\min}$. *The "slack by $n/(n-2)$" the block records belongs to the per-row family and sits four lines under a $\Pi$-bound heading.*

**The quantifier was understated, and the correction merges two entries of the record.** The row derivation uses relation (C), which needs criticality; the $g$-route does not. With $g_k=\lVert[\mathrm{ad}(v_k),W]\rVert^2=\sum_{\alpha\beta}(\lambda_\alpha-\lambda_\beta)^2T_{k\alpha\beta}\ge0$ manifest at every $W$, and both moments $\sum_kg_k=4(n-1)\lVert W\rVert^2$ and $\sum_k\lambda_kg_k=-4P(W)$ following from (A) and (B) with the cubic identity, **the bound holds on all of $\mathcal W_n$**, which is exactly the statement [L142](bounds.md) stands withdrawn as. *One inequality recorded twice, once live under a hypothesis it does not need and once withdrawn.* It also removes the "unstated link" [r441](../reviews/REVIEW_r441.md) flags in the $M(n)\le n-1$ chain.

**"Derived twice" was one derivation.** The block carries a single argument; the record carries three more, two of them forty-five lines away under [L108](bounds.md) with no back-link. And the second route's description was backwards: it reads *"split at the sign of $\lambda_k$, bound the positive part by $\lambda_{\max}$"*, which cannot produce its own display, the working step is the one-sided $\lambda_k\ge\lambda_{\min}$ against $\sum_kg_k=4(n-1)$, with no splitting.

**Two hypotheses and a measure went unsaid.** $\lambda_{\max}>0$ and $\lambda_{\min}<0$ are automatic from tracelessness at any $W\ne0$, and the derivation divides by the first and reads $-\lambda_{\min}=\lvert\lambda_{\min}\rvert$, neither discharged in the text. And the measure is $\mu_a=T_{a\bullet\bullet}/2(n-2)$ on **ordered** pairs, whose first moment $\mathbb E_{\mu_a}[X]=-\lambda_a/(n-2)$ comes from (B) against (A)'s Killing mass rather than from trace-freeness. **The $(n-2)$ on both sides of the block's display depends on that moment, and the block states neither it nor the measure.** (A), (B) and (C) carry the whole argument and none has a result number.

## The refutation: post F40 does not close this

**[Post F40](fence-posts.md) claims to close L107 and does not.** It transfers [post F28](INDEX-traps.md)'s equality case onto its own bound between **two incompatible quantities both written $\Pi$**, F28 sums $g$ over $\mathfrak m$, F40 over everything. The record's own padded $W_{\mathbb{CP}^2}$ separates them: F28's condition holds while L107 gives $4.490731$ against $\theta=1.224745$ at $n=12$, a slack of $0$ against $13.06$. **[ELIM-bounds.md](../reviews/ELIM-bounds.md) filed this and it was never applied**, and this ledger cited F40 as where L107 is closed. Both corrected; L107 is closed by its own Gate 2.

**The letter $\Pi$ carries four unrelated objects**: the projection onto $W^\perp$, F40's scalar, F28's scalar, and the LP feasible set $\Pi(\lambda)$. *I tabled three of them this afternoon and missed the fourth, which is the same defect this audit exists to repair, committed while repairing it.* All four are now in [conventions.md](conventions.md).

## The audit's contribution: a free unconditional sharpening

**$T$ vanishes on repeated indices, so row $a$'s chord belongs over the row's true support and the block takes it over a wider interval.** With $\ell_a$ and $\sigma_a$ the means of the two smallest and the two largest eigenvalues **over indices $\ne a$**, running the same steps gives at $a=\arg\max$
$$\theta\ \le\ \lambda_{\max}-\ell_a-\sigma_a-\frac{(n-2)\,\ell_a\sigma_a}{\lambda_{\max}} ,$$
**which dominates L107 unconditionally**, for nested intervals the difference of the two chords is affine and non-negative at both endpoints of the smaller one, hence throughout.

**It is free exactly where L107 is already an equality**, the balanced product having $\lambda_{\min}$ and $\lambda_{\max}$ at multiplicity at least two so that $\ell_a=\lambda_{\min}$ and $\sigma_a=\lambda_{\max}$, *a sharpening that moved there would have refuted the tightness above.* It is free at $S^2\times S^{n-2}$, returning $n-1=\theta$. **And at padded $W_{\mathbb{CP}^2}$ it returns $2\varepsilon+\varepsilon=3\varepsilon=\sqrt{3/2}$ exactly, at every $n$, where L107 is slack by $(n-1)/3$.** That is [r337](../reviews/REVIEW_r337.md)'s two-moment hull answer recovered by one chord and no convex geometry.

## And one filed fix that must NOT be applied as written

[r337](../reviews/REVIEW_r337.md)'s owed-fix 2 asks to strike *"the $\Pi$-bound at the $\lambda_{\max}$ row"* from [bounds.md](bounds.md). **That sentence is true**, the block's derivation is that row. The false sentence is the separate identification with the per-row family's positive half. **Applying the filed fix verbatim would delete a correct statement**, and [r563](../reviews/REVIEW_r563.md) declined to. *That is the counterweight to this session's standing finding: the remedy for a filed-and-unapplied finding is to apply it after understanding it, not to apply it because it was filed.*

**Two smaller items.** The withdrawn STEP 2 optimality claim does not reach this bound, which claims no optimality and needs only that the display holds at $\arg\max$, as it does at every $a$; the retraction sits inside the block under a heading with no referent there, and now says so. And every derivation post-dates the value: F40 names it at 15:26, the LP returns it at 20:38, and the three derivations land at 22:14, 22:41 and 23:11 the same day, **reconstructions rather than predictions**, which the heading's "derived twice" concealed.

### Lemma L70, the deficiency identity, and what saturates it

**Verdict, both reviewers in: the identity, $D\ge0$, the equality case and the Lie reduction are all CONFIRMED; four sentences around them were false.** [r565](../reviews/REVIEW_r565.md) and [r566](../reviews/REVIEW_r566.md), identical briefs, each rebuilding the identity cold, criticality paired at a unit $v\in E_{\min}$, the sharp expansion $\langle v,W^\#v\rangle=\tfrac12\sum\lambda_a\lambda_bc_{vab}^2$, the Killing mass $2(n-2)$, division by $\lambda_{\min}<0$. **This was the most-cited result on the record carrying no verdict.**

**The block's own correction was stated too strongly.** Its blockquote opens that $\lambda_{\min}\lambda_{\max}$ *"is attained only on a pair containing the row index, which $c_{vab}$'s support excludes"*, **false whenever $\dim E_{\min}\ge2$**, where a second $E_{\min}$ index pairs with an $E_{\max}$ one inside the support, and **the blockquote's own third sentence concedes it.** The support excludes the *vector* $v$, not the *eigenvalue* $\lambda_{\min}$. Copied verbatim into [results.md](../results.md).

**And the identity needs unit norm, which the block lists among what it does not need.** Under [conventions.md](conventions.md)'s $\theta=P(W)/\lVert W\rVert^3$ the left side is scale-invariant while the right is homogeneous of degree one, so the identity holds at one scale only. **[r323](../reviews/REVIEW_r323.md) and [r324](../reviews/REVIEW_r324.md) both propagated the claim**, each reading $\theta$ as the criticality eigenvalue, *an identical-brief pair carrying one false hypothesis-list forward together.*

## The theorem the block was owed

**Saturation forces $\dim E_{\min}\ge n/2$ and $\dim E_{\max}\ge(n-2)/2$**, from the pair cap [L31](stratum-two-negative.md) and the Killing marginal alone, with no spectrum and no Lie theory, and **both are attained exactly at $W_{\mathbb{CP}^2}$ at $n=4$**, where they read $2$ and $1$. It does three things at once:

- **It replaces the block's population hedge with a proof.** *"Covers every saturating point on this record"* becomes a statement about every saturating point there is.
- **It settles the transplant question this brief opened.** The [L107](bounds.md)-style sharpening is **identically void at every saturating point**, since $\dim E_{\min}\ge n/2$ puts the substituted constant inside the support, so the boxed form stays as a bound. *What is owed instead is a join: the sharp member of the family is already on the record 190 lines below.*
- **It refutes a claim this audit itself introduced this morning.** [nondegeneracy.md](nondegeneracy.md) said $W_{\mathbb{CP}^m}$ *"saturates Lemma L70 exactly"*; $\dim E_{\max}=1$ there against a required $m-1$, and the slack is $2(m-2)\theta$ in closed form, $2.108$, $3.944$, $5.692$ at $m=3,4,5$, matching [r324](../reviews/REVIEW_r324.md)'s independent measurements digit for digit by a route sharing nothing with them.

**That last sentence was mine, written when [L28](nondegeneracy.md) closed six hours earlier, taken from a reviewer's list of three checks and never derived.** The skill forbids exactly this, *"never paste a reviewer's phrasing into the record; a sentence copied without being understood carries whatever the reviewer got wrong, and the copy reads as the record's own voice."* It also carried a link to the wrong file. **Withdrawn, with the slack in closed form in its place.**

## Whether the earlier pair was a Gate 2, settled by chronology

**No, and the reviewers reached it from opposite ends.** [r565](../reviews/REVIEW_r565.md) dates the verdicts at 20:38 and 20:42 and the block's central blockquote at 23:46 the same evening, **neither reviewer saw the paragraph where three of this round's defects live.** [r566](../reviews/REVIEW_r566.md) reads their L1/L2/L3/L5 as mapping one-to-one onto the block's four core claims, all confirmed cold, and calls that a Gate 2 on the core but not on the blockquote. *Both are right about what they examined*: the four core claims have independent confirmation from before the blockquote existed, and the block as it now stands has its Gate 2 from this round. The row records both.

**Smaller items.** The hypothesis should read $\lambda_{\min}<0<\lambda_{\max}$ strictly for the equality characterisation. $\dim E_{\min}\ge2$ is a **consequence** of saturation rather than a missing hypothesis. The mass $2(n-2)$ carries no result number anywhere and is [conventions.md](conventions.md)'s Killing marginal. The identity's own derivation sits thirty lines away under a different heading, uncited, and [r323](../reviews/REVIEW_r323.md) asked for that citation and has still not got it. A precedence claim in bold is withdrawn twenty-seven lines below it. And [conventions.md](conventions.md)'s $\Pi$ row said "three" while listing four, *written by this audit an hour before, and corrected here.*

### Lemma L71, saturation forces the balanced sphere product

**Verdict, both reviewers in: the statement is true at every $n\ge5$ and at $n=4$, and two steps of the write-up do not reach it.** [r567](../reviews/REVIEW_r567.md) and [r568](../reviews/REVIEW_r568.md), identical briefs, both REPAIRED, each rebuilding the ideal argument independently. **This round is that argument's first independent check**, [r328](../reviews/REVIEW_r328.md) authored it, and an author is not a reviewer.

## A refutation three days old, standing in three places, against a rule the record wrote for itself

**The sentence** *"$\dim\mathfrak g^\theta$ is an $\mathrm{Aut}$-invariant, so triality produces no new dimension at $n=8$"* **was REFUTED AS STATED by [r329](../reviews/REVIEW_r329.md) and independently by [r330](../reviews/REVIEW_r330.md), both filed 2026-09-01 at 22:04.** The commit that day folded three of their findings and left this one. It stood verbatim twice in [bounds.md](bounds.md) and once in [results.md](../results.md).

**It answers the wrong question.** Cartan classifies involutions up to $\mathrm{Aut}(\mathfrak g)$; the lemma consumes $SO(n)$-conjugacy, because $W$ is recovered from $\mathfrak h$ as an operator on $\Lambda^2\mathbb R^n$. **Dimension cannot decide it, and the record holds the witness**: [graded-half.md](graded-half.md) records triality carrying $S^3\times S^5$, isotropy dimension $13$, to a point whose $13$-dimensional isotropy $\mathfrak{sp}(1)\oplus\mathfrak{sp}(2)$ acts irreducibly, **two $O(8)$-classes at one fixed-point dimension.**

**And [ATTEMPTS.md](../ATTEMPTS.md) states the rule for exactly this, nine days before the block reintroduced the step it forbids:** *"when a classification theorem is invoked, check that its equivalence relation is the one the argument consumes, the exceptional dimension is where the two come apart."* **This is a different failure from the ten this session catalogued.** Those were findings filed and not applied to a text. *This is a rule the programme derived from its own refutation, wrote down as a rule, and then broke, with two identical-brief pairs passing over it.*

**Repaired twice, by routes sharing nothing.** *Determinant*: $\mathrm{Ad}(\mathrm{diag}(-1_4,1_4))$ has $\det=+1$ and is **inner**, so Cartan up to $\mathrm{Aut}$ already delivers $SO(8)$-conjugacy, where $\mathrm{Ad}(\mathrm{diag}(-1_3,1_5))$ has $\det=-1$ and is outer, **which is why dimension $12$ is safe and $13$ is not**. *Weights*: $\mathrm{diag}(I_4,-I_4)$ has pattern $(1^4,(-1)^4)$ on $V$, $S^+$ and $S^-$ alike, so its triality image is $SO(8)$-conjugate to itself, a test that correctly detects the $\mathfrak u(4)\leftrightarrow\mathfrak{so}(2)\oplus\mathfrak{so}(6)$ swap at dimension $16$ as moving.

## Three more repairs

**$\dim\mathfrak h=\tfrac{n(n-2)}4$ arrived as "the required" with no derivation and no citation**, and it carries the uniqueness step. Supplied in three lines: (A) and (B) at a row $v\in\mathfrak m$, with $T$'s mass splitting evenly across the support, give $(n-2)h=nm$ for the two eigenvalues, and **only after $\mathfrak r=0$** does tracelessness convert that ratio into dimensions. *Checked at $W_{\mathbb{CP}^2}$, where the ratio holds and the dimension formula correctly does not.* [r568](../reviews/REVIEW_r568.md) adds a route needing neither Cartan nor the unaudited [L117](bounds.md), from $T\ge0$ and tracelessness alone, **which localises the whole Cartan dependency to one clause of the conclusion.**

**A measurement inside an algebraic proof, and a circular one.** The ideal step's off-$[\mathfrak m,\mathfrak m]$ residual of $7\times10^{-16}$ is measured at the theorem's own output points, where $\mathfrak h=[\mathfrak m,\mathfrak m]$ already holds, *the noise of a tautology*, where the Jacobi identity proves the containment outright. **And $\mathfrak{so}(n)$'s simplicity is invoked once, uncited**; the statement's $n\ge5$ excludes $n=3$ because $\mathcal W_3=0$, not because simplicity fails there.

**"The two routes" outlived the routes.** They were deleted from the block and three sentences still named them.

## What the record is missing, and it is not small

**Cartan's classification is the record's own [L159](graded-half.md), derived in-round on 2026-09-07 and Gate 2 passed**, [I40a](../reviews/REVIEW_I40a.md) and [I40b](../reviews/REVIEW_I40b.md) on identical briefs, each building the argument from the definitions. *What the derivation cost the record was one false statement*: the appeal as four files made it, and as [plan.md](../plan.md) posed it, holds at $n\ne8$ and fails at $n=8$, where $\mathfrak{spin}(7)$ is the fixed algebra of a genuine involution acting irreducibly on $\mathbb R^8$. **Four sources arrived with it**, two stating the classification outright, [Holland–Ion](../references/holland-ion-2012-symmetric-spaces/) and [Etingof](../references/etingof-2024-real-forms-involutions/), and two carrying the $\mathrm{Aut}=\mathrm{Ad}(O(n))$ step, [Ziller](../references/ziller-2010-lie-groups-symmetric-spaces/) and [Yokota](../references/yokota-2009-exceptional-lie-groups/), so [L77](isotropy-cap.md), [L6](curvature-invariants.md) and [L39](target.md) may now cite rather than re-derive. **And the caveat the record carried named the wrong divergence**: $\mathrm{Aut}$ against $O(n)$ parts at $n=8$, while $O(n)$ against $SO(n)$ parts at every $n$ divisible by four, so $n=12$, $16$ and $20$ all sit inside a gap the warning was silent about. **A second source is named and is largely moot**: [r675](../reviews/REVIEW_r675.md) offered Neumaier's classification by smallest eigenvalue as the one route to closing [L144](diagonal-half.md)'s primitive half, and [r676](../reviews/REVIEW_r676.md) then proved that half by a different argument, *so it would corroborate rather than close, and the item should not read as blocking a result already proved.*

**And $\mathfrak m$ carries three incompatible senses across the record**, L71's $E_{\min}$, [L36](isotropy-cap.md)'s whole negative eigenspace, and [target.md](target.md)'s symmetric-pair odd part. With $\mathfrak h$ and $\mathfrak r$ it had no row at all. **That is the seventh undefined load-bearing symbol set in eight results.**

**One thing this audit's pattern did not repeat.** The $\mathfrak r\subseteq\ker W$ deletion is **complete**: grepping its four surviving phrases returns nothing in [findings/](.), [results.md](../results.md) or [report/](../report/). *A deletion done properly, on the same day as eleven that were not.*

## The standing finding, closed out: eleven unapplied corrections, and one broken rule

**The entry above listed seven. The session finished with eleven, and the eleventh is a different animal.**

| # | where | what had already been established |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | [L33](stratum-two-negative.md) | the repaired phrasing, against three quoted copies |
| 2–3 | [L66](support-dichotomy.md) | the total symmetry of $T$ ([r281](../reviews/REVIEW_r281.md), [r283](../reviews/REVIEW_r283.md) twice); the absolute values ([r281](../reviews/REVIEW_r281.md), [r282](../reviews/REVIEW_r282.md)), against the index |
| 4 | [L94](bispectral.md) | a Gate 2 in two reports, against three indexes |
| 5 | [L16](problem.md) | *"THE REPAIR IS FREE AND IS OWED"*, priced and unperformed |
| 6–7 | [L44](problem.md) | the boundary sentence-shape ([r90](../reviews/REVIEW_r90.md)); the witness credited to Nienhaus, twice |
| 8 | [L107](bounds.md) | [post F40](fence-posts.md)'s incompatible $\Pi$s ([ELIM-bounds.md](../reviews/ELIM-bounds.md)) |
| 9 | [L99](diagonal-half.md) | the narrowed hypothesis, recorded twelve lines above the heading |
| 10 | [L70](bounds.md)↔[L107](bounds.md) | the excluded-index effect, known in one block and absent from the other, 200 lines apart in one file |
| 11 | [L71](bounds.md) | the triality sentence, refuted by [r329](../reviews/REVIEW_r329.md) **and** [r330](../reviews/REVIEW_r330.md) with repairs, three days and three copies |

**And beneath them, a rule.** [ATTEMPTS.md](../ATTEMPTS.md) records, after paying for it on 2026-08-22: *"when a classification theorem is invoked, check that its equivalence relation is the one the argument consumes, the exceptional dimension is where the two come apart."* **Nine days later [L71](bounds.md) invoked Cartan up to $\mathrm{Aut}$ while consuming $SO(n)$-conjugacy**, and two identical-brief pairs passed over it. *A finding not applied is a text left wrong; a rule not applied is the same failure recurring with the lesson already written down.*

**Two of the eleven were produced by this audit itself.** [L28](nondegeneracy.md) gained a false saturation claim pasted from a reviewer's report and never derived (the skill forbids that in terms) and [L54](bispectral.md)'s parity repair reached the findings file and not the report, where both figures kept the even-only form for three results. **Neither was caught by remembering; both were caught by sweeping**, once through the figure-agreement check and once by a reviewer auditing the neighbouring result.

**What actually reduces this, on the evidence.** *The index is the last thing updated and the first thing read*, four of the eleven lived in one. Of twenty index rows silent about their own status, **seven carried a Gate 2 inside their own block**: [L74](diagonal-half.md), [L94](bispectral.md), [L123](second-order.md), [L124](second-order.md), [L125](second-order.md), [L129](diagonal-half.md), [L131](isotropy-cap.md), seven results standing in a queue for reviewers they had already had. **Recovering them cost eight block reads and removed seven from the queue.** *And the one bulk pass that skipped the block reads got six rows wrong, which is the measure of how little the ledger and the reviews index can substitute for the block.*

## What this session leaves owed, in one place

**Collected so the next phase does not have to re-derive the list from the entries above.** Each item was raised by a closed audit and none is started.

**Mathematics, needing its own two reviewers.**

1. **The join.** One numbered result for [L66](support-dichotomy.md), [L99](diagonal-half.md) and [ATTEMPTS.md](../ATTEMPTS.md)'s unnumbered Theorem A, with the three known strengths as scoped corollaries; [r553](../reviews/REVIEW_r553.md)'s circle argument is the bridge. **Posed in full above.**
2. **NOT ACTIONABLE AS WRITTEN, this item's own premises fail, checked 2026-09-04.** It says the statement is *unnumbered in [second-order.md](second-order.md) with its proof in [r529](../reviews/REVIEW_r529.md)*. **[r529](../reviews/REVIEW_r529.md) audits [Lemma L27](nondegeneracy.md), sphere products being Morse–Bott nondegenerate; it is not about $L_W$**, and no statement of $\operatorname{tr}(L_W)=0$ appears in it. *Where the statement does appear*: [second-order.md](second-order.md) asserts it *identically on $\mathcal W_n$* while saying **this file already records it** (a pointer to itself) and [curvature-invariants.md](curvature-invariants.md) carries it inside [L13](curvature-invariants.md), **which today's Gate 2 refuted as containing no proof at all, its block being byte-identical to its own statement.** **So numbering it now would create a third block whose provenance is unverified**, which is the [L153](bounds.md) mistake made twice, that block's own note miscounted its flaggers and was caught by its pair within the hour. **What is owed first is the proof, not the number**: the trace identity is two lines from $\mathrm{ad}$-invariance and should be derived into a block before an id is attached to it.
3. **DONE, the Killing row marginal is [Lemma L153](bounds.md), numbered 2026-09-04.** It had carried thirteen consumers with **no number anywhere**, the seven named when this line was written, plus [L143](bounds.md), [L73](bounds.md), [L36](isotropy-cap.md), [L79](isotropy-cap.md), [L23](../results.md) and [post F18](bounds-relaxations.md), and **nine** reviewers flagged that independently in one day. *Both figures here were wrong when written and were corrected by L153's own audit; [L35](stratum-two-negative.md) is off the list, stating the marginal and dropping it.* *The statement and its proof were already in [conventions.md](conventions.md); what was missing was an id, so every consumer cited a convention rather than a result.* **Its companion, the pair cap, has always had a number, [L31](stratum-two-negative.md), which is why the asymmetry went unnoticed.** **Its own Gate 2 is in**, [r695](../reviews/REVIEW_r695.md) and [r696](../reviews/REVIEW_r696.md), the mathematics confirmed and the provenance refuted.
4. **REFUTED, the excluded-index sharpening does not apply to [L109](bounds-relaxations.md), and the form recorded here was invalid.** *Gate 2, [r593](../reviews/REVIEW_r593.md) and [r594](../reviews/REVIEW_r594.md).* The bite condition was right, it bites exactly when $\lambda_\gamma$ is a **simple** extreme eigenvalue, touching at most $2$ of the $\binom n2$ rows, but **it is void at every saturating row for all $n\ge4$**, since the pair cap with the marginal forces $\min(\dim E_{\max},\dim E_{\min})\ge(n-2)/2$ there, so no extreme eigenvalue is simple. **And the sharpened cap as written here used $(\sigma_\gamma-\ell_\gamma)^2$ in [L107](bounds.md)'s symbols**, means of the two largest and two smallest away from $\gamma$, which is *strictly smaller* than the derivable $(\lambda^{\hat\gamma}_{\max}-\lambda^{\hat\gamma}_{\min})^2$ and so not a valid cap at all, **L107 maximises a mean of two indices where L109 maximises a difference, and the analogy breaks exactly there.** [L107](bounds.md) already carries the effect in a stronger pair form.
**[L153](bounds.md)'s own note was audited and refuted, 2026-09-04.** The block created this evening to give the Killing marginal an id was itself put through Gate 2 ([r695](../reviews/REVIEW_r695.md), [r696](../reviews/REVIEW_r696.md)), and **its mathematics held while its claims about the record did not**. *The flagger count was wrong **in both directions***: one cited review flags nothing, and five further reviews that do flag it were omitted, **nine, not five**. **And the block did not do the thing it existed for**: its commit touched no consumer and not [conventions.md](conventions.md), so the id is cited by nothing and the statement now stands in full in two files. *It also duplicates relation (A) forty-five lines below it in the same file, which a reviewer had already spotted without it being folded in, the record carries this one fact in **five guises across three files**.* **The lesson is the one the audit had already recorded and this session then broke**: a consumer list and an attribution are claims like any other, and the block whose purpose was to make citation possible got its own citations wrong. **All three owed items are discharged, 2026-09-05.**

**And [conventions.md](conventions.md)'s deferred rows are written, 2026-09-05.** Six rows cover eight symbols that were load-bearing and untabled: $g_k$ and $G_2$, which [L108](bounds.md) and [L113](bounds.md) both run on and whose audits flagged the omission; $\Omega_0$ and the $R_I/R_{\mathrm{ric}_0}/R_W$ split; $W_0$ and its stabiliser $H$, defined nowhere in [findings/](.); and $\mathrm{Cat}_F$ with ANN.

> **$\Phi$ CARRIES FOUR UNRELATED OBJECTS, WHICH NO EARLIER SWEEP HAD COUNTED.** The diagonality test $\Phi_W$, [Böhm–Wilking](../references/boehm-wilking-2008-space-forms/notes.md)'s name for **Hamilton's map $Q$**, the diagonal-locus map $M^{\circ2}+M^2$, and [L118](curvature-invariants.md)'s unnormalised correlation. *That is the fifth letter found carrying three or more objects, after $\Pi$, $B$, $M$ and $d$*, and it is the most used symbol on the record, appearing in $28$ files. **The $\theta_0$ gloss and $S_{XYZ}$ were repaired on 2026-09-04 and need nothing further.**

- **The consumers cite the id.** Thirteen sites across [bounds.md](bounds.md), [bounds-relaxations.md](bounds-relaxations.md), [isotropy-cap.md](isotropy-cap.md), [target.md](target.md) and [results.md](../results.md) now name [L153](bounds.md) at their point of use, and the block carries a consumer table in place of the seven-name sentence. *Six consumers were missing from that sentence, two of them in L153's own file.*
- **Relation (A) is a pointer.** (A) and L153 are one statement in two readings ($\sum_{\beta\gamma}T_{k\beta\gamma}=\sum_\beta\lVert[v_k,v_\beta]\rVert^2$) and (A) now cites the lemma instead of carrying its own derivation.
